## 12. SEC3 day-two amendment

Implementer: SEC3, 2026-10-19. Commission: day-two fix list 3/4/5/8.
REL5 owns release transitions and compatibility; MIG owns backfills and staging
clock control. This amendment changes security analysis, not runtime authority.

### 12.1 Column read intent

`invariant table.column read only P` protects the value, computations over it,
value-dependent counts/existence/refusals, copied values, assets and delivery.
It does not forbid observing an otherwise public row's existence independently
of the protected value. Binding validates the column and preserves its identity
in every verdict and audit. Effective payload authority is row read AND any
owner-private field gate. Pure projections excluding the field may eliminate
that payload path; predicates, group/order keys, derived values and copied
fields remain observations. Unknown flow stays unproved. A synthetic whole-row
read cannot refute a projection-only column policy: column counterexamples
execute an authored observer in two schema-valid worlds differing only in that
column, with policy false in both. Outputs must differ reproducibly. Maintained
values are recomputed; column-sensitive counts/existence are included. Separate
obligations name unresolved raw-probe, wire, device and opaque flows. A direct
column theorem never silently clears those obligations. Per-column audit
reports effective audience plus operation/derived observers, not only its type.

### 12.2 Reproducible campaigns and reachability

Generated seed ingress contains only authored base rows/fields. Never re-seed
maintained targets or engine-recorded fields. Generated lifetime samples are
live at the campaign clock; principal fields vary independently. Rejected
snapshots are recorded as unsupported, not successful attempts. Existing
fixture histories instead fork the complete SQLite store and asset ledger into
owned isolated memory stores; captures, deadlines, maintained values, lifetimes
and actual media identities survive unchanged. No source store is modified.
Temporary asset directories belong to Store and disappear on drop.

Actors include all principal values in fixtures. Required/optional references
range over fixture rows, not only the first. Short histories visit first steps
breadth first before extending them under a reported work cap. Read and row
assertions are checked after each step. Write/operation assertions judge only
actual committed changes, correct insert/update/delete arms and pre-write
policy state; ambiguous multiple writes, implicit effects or unstable policy
need executed-effect tracing and remain unsupported. A finite trace is never
an induction proof for always or arbitrary histories.

Every counterexample has a machine-readable origin: authored trace from trusted
fixture, trusted-seed-only, or synthetic operation. Only the first establishes
application reachability from the recorded trusted starting state and fails
normal check/test. Seed/synthetic witnesses remain refutations of all-snapshot
claims, visible with --all and JSON, without failing acceptance. Unproved
reachability is never asserted unreachable as a mathematical theorem. Check
loads the same fixture base for reachable histories as test, without HTTP or
business-case execution. A stronger witness must replace a synthetic witness
for the same declaration. --all exposes all witnesses and never changes which
ones fail. No unreachable refutation may print pass/ok beside exit 1.

### 12.3 Actionable authority advice

N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION identifies authored caller operations that write role
or group grants trusted by later rules while the effective admission does not
establish independent authority to confer that particular grant. Include
self-signup roles and transitions where a caller can confer a role they lack.
A trusted service seat or an independently authorized administrator may appoint
other roles; holding an unrelated ordinary role does not establish delegation.
Advice names the operation, grant and gate and points to guide staging's
operator first-admin recipe. It is advice, never an implicit runtime refusal
or a proof of malicious intent. Legitimate open enrollment can be intentional.

N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE requires an actually mutable/revocable current-role
population and a retained principal/grant branch lacking that role recheck;
cover read AND write branches. Immutable/static role populations do not fire.
N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE excludes public populations and caller-owned role lookups;
only an absent/hidden distinction crossing a narrower private audience warrants
advice. Every security note has a real source location and contributes to the
reported note count. Rule printing preserves connective precedence. Identity
429 responses include a conservative retry delay covering the rate window;
the existing limits and authentication semantics are unchanged.

### 12.4 Evidence

Qualification uses read-only copies of all ten wave-17 high lanes and their
spec-authored invariant sets, plus all 150 earlier schemas. Report caught,
partial and not caught separately; all-snapshot witnesses alone do not count
as a reachable detection. Preserve bad/fixed controls, note counts and source
hashes. The open qualification-gap ticket remains open for unresolved whole
proof/device/opaque obligations. No zero-hole claim follows from finite tests.


### 12.5 SEC3 reduction details

The direct column theorem is `(row_read AND owner_private_gate) => P` in the
existing three-valued kernel; deny-delete is never an admission for column
read. Whole proof still requires every observer obligation. This increment
uses dynamic column-world observers, not an unimplemented static dataflow
claim: pure projections can avoid a refutation, but do not acquire a proof by
absence of finite counterexamples. Census is existing point/scan/view/filter/
count/sum/exists, mutation return/refusal/copy, asset, sync, raw/derived/opaque
paths. Unsupported field flow remains unproved. Audit's `column_audiences`
maps every visible column name to its effective row-and-private-field rule;
operations and derived edges remain separately enumerated.

Column worlds use the same ordinary viewer, server clock and row id; the named
column has two different schema-valid typed values, and policy is false on its
respective image in each committed world. Every non-native authored query and
deterministic mutation is eligible; fresh ids, effects and incomplete/resource
results are declined. Compare typed logical data and authored refusal messages,
retain ordered arrays, repeat both observations to reject unstable outputs.
Rebuild maintained rows from authored inputs, never accept fabricated derived
or recorded fields. Required media substitutions without a coupled real ledger
remain explicitly unsupported. A primary observation from the exact trusted
fixture is a reachable observation; its synthetic alternate world establishes
value dependency, not reachability of an alternate application history. JSON
retains both worlds and explicitly states this distinction.

`AuthoredFixtureTrace`, `TrustedSeedOnly`, `SyntheticOperation` are the witness
origins (serialized in kebab-case). Status remains refuted for an all-snapshot
counterexample. Normal text labels seed/synthetic refutations non-failing and
hides their large witnesses; --all reveals them and does not change exit status.
Only authored fixture observations fail check/test. A reachable witness wins
over a synthetic witness for the same declaration. With no fixture, check uses
schema-only exploration and names missing application-reachability coverage.
Check discovers an explicit --fixture or project fixtures/; it uses an isolated
in-memory loader at the fixed campaign clock, never the development store.

Candidates sort by operation, argument value and principal. Enumerate bounded
Cartesian arguments including nullable enums and fixture references; interleave
operations for breadth-first first steps, then diagonal ordered pairs. The
512 first-step/512-pair and 1000-fixture-row caps are reported; no exhaustive
claim is made for unvisited combinations. Fixture asset directories are owned
by Store and removed on drop, including error returns. Histories judge direct
single-write net images only; multiple writes, dependency-changing and opaque
boundaries remain unsupported rather than fabricating write-boundary events.

The escalation advisory compares role privileges structurally, never by an
administrator role-name allowlist. Enumerate literals from a role enum or
role probes over a text roster; substitute each caller/assigned role into the
roster write gates and other table-effect gates. A possible ordinary assignment
that adds an effect the caller lacks warrants advice; explicit service-only
branches are excluded from this advisory cast, not from invariant proofs.
Literal assignment targets restrict the target set; dynamic values retain all
candidate roles. This is overapproximate advice: independent operation guards,
intentional enrollment and delegation still need specification review.
Grant-lifetime notes require a role/kind column with an authored update/upsert/
delete and a mutable role field; immutable seed-only populations are excluded.
Existence advice requires a private table lookup, an independent stricter read
policy and distinct later refusal; public and viewer-owned lookups are excluded.
Locations are exact column/rule sites where available, table sites otherwise;
the project-wide missing-intent note uses the first source's line 1.

Identity rate refusals on the existing identity routes use the existing
60-second WINDOW: Retry-After: 60, denied.retryAfterMs: 60000. This is a
conservative wait, not exposure of individual rate-key state. No limit changes.
Qualification inputs are ~/sb4-loop/wave17-repro/sec/<lane>/snapback/security.q
and read-only wave17 app copies; the report pins their hashes. The ten high
lanes are 006/011/013/015/019/021/027/040/045/048. Caught means an authored
fixture observation of the reported bad behavior; partial retains a relevant
note or synthetic witness with a missing affected path. Each of the eight real
highs is scored separately. All eight caught and no false refusals on 150 older
schemas is the target, never inferred from an incomplete run.


### 12.6 Disclosure and clock clarifications

A column observer mutation is declined if it can change the protected image,
policy dependency or an unresolved implicit writer before disclosure. A
pre-operation false policy cannot refute an authorized post-transfer return.
Column observers also run after committed history steps, preserving the
trusted initial fixture and authored prefix. All reference and principal
arguments use the same bounded fixture Cartesian cases; truncation of read
observer cases at 32 is named. Clock-only whole-row probes exclude columns.
Column tick coverage beyond the recorded campaign clock remains named.

All security fixture loading, probes, writes, paired deletions and policy
judgments use the same explicit campaign clock. Business tests keep their own
existing clock and store; security reloads trusted fixture files independently.
Raw policy probes respect both not-before and expire-at bounds. Direct delete
assertions judge removed pre-images against the stable pre-write state, never
surviving rows alone; ambiguous implicit removals remain unsupported.

Fixture forks copy already activated engine state without re-adopting a schema.
They use fresh entropy/minter and private assets, never shared live connections.
Ephemeral-hub fixtures require a separate isolated snapshot and are declined.
Structural privilege advice combines dominating safe operation requirements
with the actual insert/update/upsert gate and applicable owned-field patch gate.
A mutable roster alone is not enough for lifetime advice: require independent
current-role intent or a retained recipient column tied to a role literal
(judge, inspector, host, contractor, engineer; ordinary author attribution alone
is not a current-role contract). Deletion revokes even an immutable role field.

### 12.7 Stable judgments and direct payload witnesses

Unknown bound policy dependencies never authorize a mutation observer: such
column mutations require disclosure-boundary tracing. Pre-write assertions use
a transitive closure of maintained and cascade/set-null writes; a policy
dependency or target that may change implicitly makes the assertion unsupported.
The same rule covers insert, update, upsert and delete. Looped/opaque net images
cannot stand in for executed write events. Effective inserted authority includes
a defaulted role even when omitted from the source write.

An authored complete query returning a compiler-typed Row/Table or Page/Table
containing the protected row id is a direct payload witness at that query's
snapshot. Nested typed row returns retain their source-table identity. This
avoids inventing an invalid absent world by deleting a referenced parent.
Projected scalar/aggregate/refusal observations still require validated paired
worlds; a matching untyped id or value alone cannot attribute an exposure.

Check prioritizes actual fixture histories and column observers before searching
remaining synthetic counterexamples. It does not repeat the test-only operation
exploration matrix, which judges no additional policy. That matrix shares an
8192-call budget across every operation (16–512 calls per operation), reports
truncation, and preserves enum/reference/principal alternatives within that cap.
Required image/file operation arguments are actual uploads owned by the acting
principal, with ledger/scratch custody in the isolated engine. Unsupported video
and uncoupled column-world media identities remain named.

For escalation advice, a role-protected appointer is an explicit delegation
boundary; conferring a different role is not by itself evidence of escalation.
Independent role-value intent may restrict that boundary (for example a producer
must not confer legal). Ordinary role casts make service-seat equality false,
including inside operation requirements. Fixed literals still undergo the privilege comparison; ordinary enrollment
whose rules add no role-specific authority is not advised. Analyze caller-chosen
roles, defaulted inserted authority and owner-field upgrades; respect the
argument's actual enum choices. A fixed bootstrap that can appoint further
roles remains an actionable first-admin risk. Notes remain advisory; absence
of independent intent is not proof that a delegation matches the specification.

### 12.8 Runtime intent and independent worlds

Reference-hop intent is judged by the engine's own authorized RuleProbe and
three-valued verdict on an isolated actual snapshot. Hidden/missing references
remain Unknown, never false; snapshot-only evaluators decline those terms.
Temporal reference policies require a per-tick authorized replay and remain
explicitly unsupported until that path runs. Referenced immutable-column worlds
use fresh stores with copied verified asset ledgers and all authored base rows,
then ordinary seed validation/recomputed maintenance. They do not require a
legal delete transition. Every non-protected base field, capture, row identity
and row population must match before comparing observations.

Implicit stability includes source and member writes to row-keyed views,
maintained owner columns, through-parent effects and transitive cascade/set-null.
Only an actual direct delete can refute delete authority; commit-end expiry is
not relabeled a deletion by the caller. Defaulted upsert roles are analyzed on
the insert arm only; omitted fields are preserved on update. Revocability advice
requires an expanded write to a mutable role field or an admitted deletion,
not an unrelated display-name update.

For a write history, a stable necessary policy component may establish a
violation despite another changing conjunct. Replace unstable atoms (or an
entire unstable negation) by Allow, retaining And/Or composition; separately
verify `original => weakened` with the existing exhaustive kernel. Denial of
that weakened condition establishes denial of the original, never its proof.
Stability includes transitive read-authority dependencies of referenced rows.
Do not use this reduction when implicit writes can change the target image or
executed arm. This catches a demoted recorder whose role stays false even when
another statement updates an unrelated field on its parent. No new axiom is
introduced. Id-column presence/count intent shares the row identity observer
path; arbitrary untyped matching ids do not establish a payload witness.

Independent intent binds authorized reference operands without installing a
runtime read gate; live-hop restrictions on executable read rules do not remove
those operands from intent. Probe-key/equality reference hops participate in
stability and transitive authority dependency collection. Every committed
history state rechecks row assertions, including unchanged rows whose related
policy rows changed; pre-existing unchanged seed violations stay supplemental.
Role advice expands helpers with lexical argument substitution and dominating
requirements, retaining the actual write source site. Constant defaults and
literal assignments use the same authority comparison. Optional-column pairs
vary both values and null presence to observe value-existence aggregates.

Mutation column observers include transitive read-authority dependencies and
implicit targets when establishing policy stability. Role probes normalize
unique-key components and extra equalities identically. Argument-domain
narrowing follows the resolved argument expression, never a coincidentally
same-named argument beside a literal assignment. An unconditional helper call
exports its guaranteed requirement prefix to subsequent caller writes;
conditional/loop-local requirements do not become caller assumptions.

Column observers first judge authorized intent on the validated primary world
once per actor. They share a 16384-call campaign budget (including repeatability
and declined worlds), with explicit remaining-world coverage when exhausted.
The budget cannot discharge an obligation or turn an unproved path into proof.

     1	//! Independent security intent and conservative exposure obligations.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — a direct-rule theorem never clears an unmodeled path.
     3	use super::{catalog::Catalog, Bound};
     4	use crate::{ast, Diagnostic, Site};
     5	use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Schema, Effect};
     6	use serde::Serialize;
     7	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     8	pub mod kernel;
     9	mod audit;
    10	pub mod challenges;
    11	mod lints;
    12	mod operation;
    13	mod concrete;
    14	mod census;
    15	mod observers;
    16	mod temporal;
    17	mod adoption;
    18	mod images;
    19	mod columns;
    20	mod privilege;
    21	mod authority_walk;
    22	pub use audit::{Exposure, Grant};
    23	pub use observers::Obligation;
    24	pub use adoption::{draft, UncoveredExposure};
    25	
    26	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
    27	pub struct Invariant {
    28	    pub table: String,
    29	    pub target: String,
    30	    pub column: Option<String>,
    31	    pub predicate: String,
    32	    pub site: Site,
    33	    pub policy: Option<RuleExpr>,
    34	    pub operation: Option<String>,
    35	    pub requirements: Vec<RuleExpr>,
    36	    #[serde(skip)]
    37	    pub syntax: Option<ast::Expr>,
    38	    pub unsupported: Option<String>,
    39	}
    40	impl Invariant {
    41	    pub fn target_label(&self)->String {match &self.column{Some(c)=>format!("{}.{} {}",self.table,c,self.target),None=>format!("{}.{}",self.table,self.target)}}
    42	}
    43	pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    44	    let mut out=Vec::new();
    45	    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
    46	        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
    47	            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
    48	        };
    49	        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
    50	        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
    51	            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"&&!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
    52	            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
    53	        }
    54	        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
    55	        // Independent intent may use authorized reference operands even when a
    56	        // runtime read gate must use captured authority. It never installs a gate.
    57	        let effect=if effect==ast::Effect::Read{ast::Effect::Insert}else{effect};
    58	        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
    59	        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
    60	            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
    61	                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
    62	                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
    63	            },
    64	        };
    65	        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
    66	        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    67	    }
    68	    out
    69	}
    70	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    71	#[serde(rename_all="lowercase")]
    72	pub enum Status { Proved, Refuted, Unproved }
    73	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
    74	pub struct Verdict {
    75	    pub invariant: Invariant,
    76	    pub status: Status,
    77	    pub direct_rule: Status,
    78	    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    79	    pub reasons: Vec<String>,
    80	    pub counterexample: Option<challenges::Counterexample>,
    81	    pub obligations: Vec<Obligation>,
    82	}
    83	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
    84	pub struct Report {
    85	    pub format: u32,
    86	    pub scope: String,
    87	    pub exclusions: Vec<String>,
    88	    pub invariants: Vec<Verdict>,
    89	    pub exposures: Vec<Exposure>,
    90	    pub roles: BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>,
    91	    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    92	    pub uncovered: Vec<UncoveredExposure>,
    93	    pub campaign_coverage: BTreeSet<String>,
    94	}
    95	impl Report {
    96	    pub fn refuted(&self)->bool {self.invariants.iter().any(|v|v.status==Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability==challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace))}
    97	    pub fn text(&self)->String {self.text_with_all(false)}
    98	    pub fn text_with_all(&self,all:bool)->String {
    99	        let mut out=format!("security: {}\n",self.scope);
   100	        for v in &self.invariants {
   101	            if !all&&v.status==Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability!=challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace){out.push_str(&format!("  {}: seed/synthetic refutation only (non-failing; --all for details)\n",v.invariant.target_label()));continue;}
   102	            out.push_str(&format!("  {:?}: {}.{} {} — direct rule {:?}\n",v.status,v.invariant.table,format!("{}{}",v.invariant.column.as_ref().map(|c|format!("{c} ")).unwrap_or_default(),v.invariant.target),v.invariant.predicate,v.direct_rule));
   103	            for reason in &v.reasons {out.push_str(&format!("    {reason}\n"));}
   104	            for o in &v.obligations {out.push_str(&format!("    {:?}: {} — {}\n",o.status,o.path,o.reason));if let Some(repair)=&o.repair{out.push_str(&format!("      repair: {repair}\n"));}}
   105	            if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample {out.push_str(&format!("    counterexample {}\n",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap()));}
   106	        }
   107	        for note in &self.notes {out.push_str(&format!("  {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite));}
   108	        for (role,entries) in &self.roles {
   109	            out.push_str(&format!("role {role} (possible/conditional; row predicates below decide):\n"));
   110	            for entry in entries {out.push_str(&format!("  {entry}\n"));}
   111	        }
   112	        for x in &self.exposures {
   113	            out.push_str(&format!("table {} columns: {}\n",x.table,x.columns.iter().map(|(c,v)|format!("{c} ({v})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
   114	            for (column,audience) in &x.column_audiences {out.push_str(&format!("  column {column} audience: {audience}\n"));}
   115	            for path in &x.paths {out.push_str(&format!("  {path}\n"));}
   116	            for grant in &x.grants {out.push_str(&format!("  inherited grant {}: {}\n",grant.table,grant.predicate)); for w in &grant.writers {out.push_str(&format!("    writer {w}\n"));}}
   117	        }
   118	        for reason in &self.campaign_coverage{out.push_str(&format!("  campaign not covered: {reason}\n"));}
   119	        out.push_str(&format!("  exclusions: {}\n",self.exclusions.join("; ")));
   120	        out.push_str("exposures without an invariant (conditional role/row coverage; declarations are not proof):\n");
   121	        for x in &self.uncovered {out.push_str(&format!("  {}.{}: {}; rows: {}\n",x.table,x.effect,x.audience,x.row_predicate));}
   122	        if self.uncovered.is_empty(){out.push_str("  none\n");}out
   123	    }
   124	}
   125	fn effect(word:&str)->Effect {match word {"read"=>Effect::Read,"insert"=>Effect::Insert,"update"=>Effect::Update,_=>Effect::Delete}}
   126	fn effects(inv:&Invariant)->Vec<&str> { if inv.target=="write" {vec!["insert","update"]} else {vec![inv.target.as_str()]} }
   127	/// Analyze declarations without using fixtures as proof assumptions.
   128	pub fn analyze(bound:&Bound)->Report {
   129	    let exposures=audit::exposures(bound);
   130	    let mut verdicts=Vec::new();
   131	    for inv in &bound.invariants {
   132	        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   133	        let mut reasons=Vec::new();let mut direct=Status::Proved;
   134	        if let Some(why)=&inv.unsupported {reasons.push(why.clone());direct=Status::Unproved;}
   135	        else if inv.target=="always" {
   136	            // This narrow theorem holds on every validated committed image,
   137	            // including seed/adoption, with no assumption about a writer.
   138	            let nonnull=match inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap() {
   139	                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))|
   140	                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null),RuleTerm::Field(f)) =>
   141	                    table.columns.get(f).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional() && !matches!(t,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Json{..}) && !table.engine_field(f)),
   142	                _=>false,
   143	            };
   144	            if !nonnull {direct=Status::Unproved; reasons.push("row assertion is not implied by the implemented required-column theorem; transitions, counts and time require an additional proof".into());}
   145	        } else if inv.operation.is_some() {
   146	            match operation::prove(bound,inv) {Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push("operation admission does not imply the policy; an actual operation challenge is required".into());},Err(e)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(e);}}
   147	        } else {
   148	            for word in effects(inv) {
   149	                let admission=if word=="update" {table.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else if word=="read" {columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else{table.rules.for_effect(effect(word)).clone()};
   150	                let implication=if word=="update"{images::update(table,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())}else{kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())};
   151	                match implication {
   152	                    Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word} admission does not imply intent over independent predicates; a concrete replay is required for refutation"));},
   153	                    Err(why)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word}: {why}"));},
   154	                }
   155	                // Update implication judges both images. Only id/explicit
   156	                // immutable local fields have the equal-image substitution;
   157	                // owner-field and implicit writers remain separate blockers.
   158	            }
   159	        }
   160	        reasons.extend(blockers(bound,inv));
   161	        let obligations=observers::obligations(bound,inv);
   162	        for o in &obligations {if o.status!=Status::Proved {reasons.push(format!("{}: {}; {}",o.path,o.reason,o.repair.as_deref().unwrap_or("declare an explicit observer policy")));}}
   163	        let paths=exposures.iter().find(|x|x.table==inv.table).map(|x|x.paths.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
   164	        let status=if direct==Status::Proved && reasons.is_empty(){Status::Proved}else{Status::Unproved};
   165	        verdicts.push(Verdict{invariant:inv.clone(),status,direct_rule:direct,paths,reasons,counterexample:None,obligations});
   166	    }
   167	    let mut notes=lints::notes(bound,&exposures);
   168	    let missing=adoption::missing(bound);
   169	    if !missing.is_empty() {notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::default(),"N_SECURITY_INTENT",format!("{} tables have access rules and no security invariants: {}; declare the spec's privacy rules as invariants (`guide security`)",missing.len(),missing.join(", ")),"review snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft against the spec; candidates are never auto-accepted").guide("security"));}
   170	    let roles=audit::roles(&exposures);
   171	    let uncovered=adoption::uncovered(bound,&exposures);
   172	    Report{format:1,scope:"logical row/authority intent; all database snapshots; ordinary authenticated viewers including service seats; explicit unproved paths".into(),
   173	        exclusions:vec!["system-principal trust boundary".into(),"physical timing, examined counts and resource/meter side channels".into(),"offline access may use a snapshot predating server revocation".into()],invariants:verdicts,exposures,roles,notes,uncovered,campaign_coverage:BTreeSet::new()}
   174	}
   175	fn blockers(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Vec<String> {
   176	    let schema=&bound.schema;let t=&schema.tables[&inv.table];let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
   177	    if inv.target=="read" && inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,policy)!=Ok(true)) {
   178	        out.insert("wire sequencing/activity metadata has no logical-existence reduction; direct row-rule proof does not establish whole-protocol noninterference".into());
   179	    }
   180	    if inv.target=="read" {
   181	        if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {
   182	            let grants=policy.disjuncts().into_iter().filter(|r|!r.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
   183	            let global=RuleExpr::Or(grants);
   184	            if !matches!(t.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) && kernel::implies(&t.rules.insert,&global)!=Ok(true) {
   185	                out.insert("insert/unique/upsert collision outcomes can reveal an existing hidden row; old-row read authorization is not proved by new-image insert authority".into());
   186	            }
   187	            if kernel::implies(&t.update_rule_judged(),&policy.old_and_next())!=Ok(true) && !matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny) {
   188	                out.insert("blind update/upsert outcomes can reveal hidden-target existence; read correspondence is unproved".into());
   189	            }
   190	        }
   191	        if !t.written_by.is_empty(){out.insert("owner-field write outcomes can observe targets outside the row read audience".into());}
   192	    }
   193	    if inv.target=="read" && t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)| matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{..}) && t.on_delete.get(c).copied().unwrap_or(snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Restrict).acts()) {
   194	        out.insert("parent deletion/referential refusal can observe this child (including default restrict and transitive closures)".into());
   195	    }
   196	    if inv.target=="read" && t.sync.is_some(){out.insert("device read uses a delivered snapshot; current server role at disconnected access is unproved".into());}
   197	    if t.through.is_some(){out.insert("inherited/captured or live-parent authority requires a separate reduction (RULES5 owns live-parent semantics)".into());}
   198	    if !schema.receipts.is_empty() || !schema.timelines.is_empty(){out.insert("receipt/timeline engine-owned paths require a separate reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());}
   199	    if t.is_expiring() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("expiry/commit-end retirement can change rows outside ordinary delete rules".into());}
   200	    if t.ephemeral.is_some() || !t.sequences.is_empty(){out.insert("ephemeral/sequence path is outside the current proof reduction".into());}
   201	    if !t.written_by.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("owned-field update bypass: table rule alone does not establish field-write authority".into());}
   202	    for (name,other) in &schema.tables {
   203	        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && other.on_delete.values().any(|a|matches!(a,snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascnl: nl: No such file or directory
nl: nl: No such file or directory
ade|snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull)) && name==&inv.table {
   204	            out.insert("incoming cascade/set-null changes bypass this table's ordinary rules".into());
   205	        }
   206	        if inv.target=="read" {
   207	            for rule in [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete] {
   208	                if kernel::dependencies(rule).contains(&inv.table) {out.insert(format!("raw rule probe/reference from {name} can observe {} independently of its read rule",inv.table));}
   209	            }
   210	        }
   211	    }
   212	    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.target==inv.table) && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") {out.insert("aggregate/view target rows are written by engine maintenance independently of their direct table rules".into());}
   213	    if !t.maintained.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("engine-maintained columns bypass ordinary application write rules".into());}
   214	    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)|p.program.native)
   215	        || !schema.effects.is_empty() || !schema.action_specs.is_empty() {
   216	        out.insert("system/job/action/native/external path requires a reviewed reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());
   217	    }
   218	    if inv.target=="read" {
   219	        for p in &bound.programs {
   220	            let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
   221	            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && !census::body(&p.program.body) {out.insert(format!("{}: scan/merge/loop or opaque IR path needs a reviewed read-disclosure reduction",p.program.name));}
   222	            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(target,_)|target!=&inv.table) {
   223	                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));
   224	            }
   225	            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
   226	        }
   227	    }
   228	    out.into_iter().collect()
   229	}
   230	
   231	pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
   232	    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
   233	    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
   234	    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
   235	        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
   236	        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
   237	    }}
   238	    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
   239	        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
   240	        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
   241	    }}
   242	}
   243	//! Finite three-valued implication, deliberately independent of database facts.
   244	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — impossible valuations overapproximate all snapshots.
   245	use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr as R, RuleTerm as T};
   246	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
   247	
   248	pub const MAX_ATOMS: usize = 10;
   249	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   250	pub enum Truth { Yes, No, Unknown }
   251	impl Truth {
   252	    fn not(self) -> Self { match self { Self::Yes => Self::No, Self::No => Self::Yes, Self::Unknown => Self::Unknown } }
   253	    fn combine(self, other: Self, and: bool) -> Self {
   254	        let decisive = if and { Self::No } else { Self::Yes };
   255	        if self == decisive || other == decisive { decisive }
   256	        else if self == Self::Unknown || other == Self::Unknown { Self::Unknown }
   257	        else { decisive.not() }
   258	    }
   259	}
   260	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
   261	enum Formula { Constant(Truth), Atom(usize), Not(Box<Self>), And(Vec<Self>), Or(Vec<Self>) }
   262	impl Formula {
   263	    fn eval(&self, values: &[Truth]) -> Truth {
   264	        match self {
   265	            Self::Constant(v) => *v, Self::Atom(n) => values[*n], Self::Not(x) => x.eval(values).not(),
   266	            Self::And(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::Yes, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), true)),
   267	            Self::Or(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::No, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), false)),
   268	        }
   269	    }
   270	}
   271	fn term(t: &T) -> T { match t { T::NextField(f) => T::Field(f.clone()), other => other.clone() } }
   272	fn atom(rule: &R) -> String {
   273	    let mut r = rule.clone();
   274	    match &mut r {
   275	        R::Eq(a,b) | R::Compare(_,a,b) => { *a=term(a); *b=term(b); }
   276	        R::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | R::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
   277	            for t in key { *t=term(t); }
   278	            for (_,t) in equalities.iter_mut() { *t=term(t); }
   279	            equalities.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
   280	        }
   281	        _ => {}
   282	    }
   283	    if let R::Eq(a,b)=&mut r && serde_json::to_string(a).unwrap()>serde_json::to_string(b).unwrap() { std::mem::swap(a,b); }
   284	    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(&r).unwrap();
   285	    // Provenance controls planning, not rule evaluation. Remove only this
   286	    // leaf's metadata, never a similarly named literal-object field.
   287	    for name in ["Exists","Created"] { if let Some(body)=json.get_mut(name).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object_mut) { body.remove("provenance"); } }
   288	    json.to_string()
   289	}
   290	fn formula(rule: &R, atoms: &mut Vec<String>, nodes: &mut usize) -> Result<Formula, String> {
   291	    *nodes+=1; if *nodes>1024 { return Err("formula exceeds 1024 nodes".into()); }
   292	    Ok(match rule {
   293	        R::Allow | R::Public {..} => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes), R::Deny => Formula::Constant(Truth::No),
   294	        R::And(xs) => Formula::And(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
   295	        R::Or(xs) => Formula::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
   296	        R::Not(x) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(x,atoms,nodes)?)),
   297	        R::Ne(a,b) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(&R::Eq(a.clone(),b.clone()),atoms,nodes)?)),
   298	        R::Eq(T::Literal(a),T::Literal(b)) => Formula::Constant(if a==b {Truth::Yes}else{Truth::No}),
   299	        R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Viewer) => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes),
   300	        other => { let key=atom(other); let n=atoms.iter().position(|a| a==&key).unwrap_or_else(|| {atoms.push(key);atoms.len()-1});
   301	            if atoms.len()>MAX_ATOMS {return Err(format!("more than {MAX_ATOMS} independent predicates"));} Formula::Atom(n) }
   302	    })
   303	}
   304	/// `Ok(true)` is a proof; `Ok(false)` is only an abstract countermodel.
   305	/// Neither limit exhaustion nor a countermodel is a concrete refutation.
   306	pub fn implies(admission: &R, policy: &R) -> Result<bool,String> {
   307	    let (mut atoms,mut nodes)=(Vec::new(),0);
   308	    let a=formula(admission,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
   309	    let p=formula(policy,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
   310	    let mut values=vec![Truth::Unknown;atoms.len()];
   311	    for mut n in 0..3usize.pow(atoms.len() as u32) {
   312	        for v in &mut values { *v=match n%3 {0=>Truth::Yes,1=>Truth::No,_=>Truth::Unknown}; n/=3; }
   313	        if a.eval(&values)==Truth::Yes && p.eval(&values)!=Truth::Yes { return Ok(false); }
   314	    }
   315	    Ok(true)
   316	}
   317	pub fn dependencies(rule: &R) -> BTreeSet<String> {
   318	    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
   319	    match rule {
   320	        R::Exists{table,key,equalities,..}|R::Created{table,key,equalities,..} => {out.insert(table.clone());for term in key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)){for (table,_) in term.references(){out.insert(table.into());}}}
   321	        R::And(xs)|R::Or(xs) => for x in xs {out.extend(dependencies(x));}, R::Not(x) => out.extend(dependencies(x)),
   322	        R::Eq(a,b)|R::Ne(a,b)|R::Compare(_,a,b) => for t in [a,b] { for (table,_) in t.references() {out.insert(table.into());} },
   323	        _=>{}
   324	    }
   325	    out
   326	}
   327	#[cfg(test)] mod tests {
   328	    use super::*;
   329	    fn a() -> R { R::Eq(T::Field("owner".into()),T::Viewer) }
   330	    #[test] fn kleene_unknown_is_neither_true_nor_false() {
   331	        assert_eq!(Truth::Unknown.not(),Truth::Unknown);
   332	        assert_eq!(Truth::No.combine(Truth::Unknown,true),Truth::No);
   333	        assert_eq!(Truth::Yes.combine(Truth::Unknown,false),Truth::Yes);
   334	        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&R::Or(vec![a(),R::Not(Box::new(a()))])).unwrap());
   335	    }
   336	    #[test] fn no_boolean_assignment_is_a_concrete_refutation() {
   337	        assert!(implies(&R::And(vec![a(),R::Deny]),&R::Deny).unwrap());
   338	        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&a()).unwrap());
   339	        assert!(implies(&a(),&R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Field("owner".into()))).unwrap());
   340	        let xs=(0..11).map(|n|R::Eq(T::Field(format!("f{n}")),T::Viewer)).collect();
   341	        assert!(implies(&R::And(xs),&a()).is_err());
   342	    }
   343	}
   344	//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
   345	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
   346	use super::*;
   347	use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
   348	type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
   349	fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
   350	    let mut expr=expr.clone();
   351	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
   352	        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
   353	        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
   354	    });expr
   355	}
   356	fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
   357	    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
   358	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
   359	        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
   360	        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
   361	            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
   362	            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
   363	            match &f.body{
   364	                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
   365	                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
   366	            }
   367	        },_=>{}
   368	    });
   369	}
   370	// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
   371	// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
   372	fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
   373	    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
   374	    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
   375	    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
   376	    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
   377	    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
   378	    let mut out=Vec::new();
   379	    for s in body{match s{
   380	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
   381	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
   382	        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
   383	        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
   384	    }}out
   385	}
   386	fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
   387	    for s in body{match s{
   388	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
   389	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
   390	            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
   391	            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
   392	        },
   393	        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
   394	        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
   395	        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{
   396	            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
   397	            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
   398	            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
   399	        },
   400	        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
   401	        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
   402	    }}
   403	}
   404	pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
   405	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
   406	    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
   407	    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
   408	}

   409	//! Partial typed row-policy interpreter, never used to establish a proof.
   410	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown/unsupported is not false.
   411	use super::*;
   412	use crate::ast::{Expr,Literal};
   413	use snapback4_core::Value;
   414	type Rows=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
   415	fn boolean(v:Option<Value>)->Option<bool>{match v{Some(Value::Bool(v))=>Some(v),_=>None}}
   416	pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
   417	fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
   418	    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
   419	    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
   420	    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
   421	    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
   422	    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
   423	    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
   424	        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
   425	        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
   426	        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
   427	    },
   428	    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=>{
   429	        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
   430	        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
   431	            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
   432	            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
   433	            return Value::int(n).ok();
   434	        }
   435	        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
   436	        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
   437	        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}
   438	        Value::Bool(match op.as_str(){"="=>a==b,"!="=>a!=b,"<"|"<="|">"|">="=>if let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b){match op.as_str(){"<"=>a<b,"<="=>a<=b,">"=>a>b,_=>a>=b}}else{return None;},_=>return None})
   439	    },
   440	    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if (name=="count"||name=="len")&&args.len()==1 => {
   441	        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
   442	        Value::Int(values.len().try_into().ok()?)
   443	    },
   444	    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if name=="sum"&&args.len()==2 => {
   445	        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
   446	        let sum=values.iter().try_fold(0i128,|sum,r|match value(&args[1],r,rows,schema,viewer)? {Value::Int(n)=>sum.checked_add(i128::from(n)),_=>None})?;
   447	        Value::int(sum).ok()?
   448	    },
   449	    Expr::Array{items,..}=>Value::Array(items.iter().map(|e|value(e,row,rows,schema,viewer)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
   450	    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
   451	        let t=schema.table(&read.table)?;
   452	        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some()||t.through.is_some()||schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some()||read.filter.is_some()||read.range.is_some()||!read.seeks.is_empty()||read.matching.is_some(){return None;}
   453	        let mut pins=Vec::new();
   454	        for key in &read.keys{let column=key.column.as_ref()?;let val=value(&key.value,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
   455	            let kind=t.columns.get(column)?;
   456	            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
   457	            let typed=match (kind.required(),&val) {
   458	                (_,Value::Null)=>kind.is_optional(),
   459	                (C::Id|C::Principal|C::Ref{..},Value::Id(_))=>true,
   460	                (C::Text{..},Value::String(_))=>true,
   461	                (C::Enum{variants},Value::String(v))=>variants.contains(v),
   462	                (C::Int|C::IntRange{..}|C::Time,Value::Int(_))=>true,
   463	                (C::Bool,Value::Bool(_))=>true,_=>false,
   464	            };
   465	            if !typed{return None;}pins.push((column,val));}
   466	        // Integrity assertions quantify over the complete raw snapshot, not
   467	        // a caller-filtered program population. Temporal/captured populations
   468	        // above remain unsupported. No solver proof uses these samples.
   469	        let selected=rows.get(&read.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|pins.iter().all(|(c,v)|r.field(c).ok()==Some(v))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
   470	        if matches!(e,Expr::Exists{..}) {Value::Bool(!selected.is_empty())}else{Value::Array(selected)}
   471	    },
   472	    _=>return None,
   473	})}

   474	//! Conservative transitive write closure for pre-write policy snapshots.
   475	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#127-stable-judgments-and-direct-payload-witnesses — implicit writes cannot establish pre-write intent.
   476	use super::*;
   477	pub(super) fn implicit(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->BTreeSet<String>{
   478	    let mut affected=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   479	    let mut implicit=BTreeSet::new();
   480	    loop{
   481	        let before=affected.len();
   482	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if affected.contains(&m.source)||m.row_key.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|affected.contains(&r.table)){
   483	            if bound.schema.table(&m.target).is_some(){implicit.insert(m.target.clone());affected.insert(m.target.clone());}
   484	            if let Some(owner)=&m.private{implicit.insert(owner.table.clone());affected.insert(owner.table.clone());}
   485	        }}
   486	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{
   487	            if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|edge.live.is_some()&&affected.contains(&edge.parent)){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
   488	            for (column,action) in &t.on_delete{
   489	            if action.writes()&&let Some(C::Ref{table})=t.columns.get(column).map(C::required)&&affected.contains(table){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
   490	        }}
   491	        if before==affected.len(){break;}
   492	    }implicit
   493	}
   494	
   495	pub(super) fn dependencies(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{
   496	    let mut deps=kernel::dependencies(rule);
   497	    loop{let before=deps.len();for name in deps.clone(){if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&name){deps.extend(kernel::dependencies(&t.rules.read));if let Some(edge)=&t.through{deps.insert(edge.parent.clone());}}}if before==deps.len(){return deps;}}
   498	}
   499	/// A stable necessary condition may refute an intent even when another
   500	/// conjunct changes. Dropping a requirement never proves the full policy.
   501	pub(super) fn necessary(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,inv:&Invariant)->Option<Invariant>{
   502	    let policy=inv.policy.as_ref()?;let implicit=implicit(bound,p);
   503	    if implicit.contains(&inv.table){return None;}
   504	    let mut changed=implicit;
   505	    changed.extend(ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table).map(|(t,_)|t));
   506	    fn weak(bound:&Bound,r:&RuleExpr,changed:&BTreeSet<String>)->RuleExpr{
   507	        match r{
   508	            RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
   509	            RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
   510	            _ if dependencies(bound,r).iter().any(|d|changed.contains(d))=>RuleExpr::Allow,
   511	            _=>r.clone(),
   512	        }
   513	    }
   514	    let weaker=weak(bound,policy,&changed);
   515	    if kernel::implies(policy,&weaker)!=Ok(true){return None;}
   516	    let mut judged=inv.clone();judged.policy=Some(weaker);Some(judged)
   517	}

    10	fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    11	    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    12	    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    13	    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    14	    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    15	    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    16	    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    17	    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    18	    _=>return None,

   102	                if a==b{continue;}
   103	                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if aa==a&&bb==b){continue;}
   104	                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:Some(altrow.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:other.clone()})});
   105	                return;
   106	            }
   107	        }
   108	    }
   109	}
   110	fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
   111	    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
   112	    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
   113	}
   114	pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
   115	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
   116	        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
   117	        for variant in 0..16{
   118	            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   119	            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   120	            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
   121	            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
   122	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
   123	        }
   124	    }
   125	}
   126	pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   127	    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   128	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
   129	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
   130	    }
   131	}
   132	
   133	pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   134	    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
   135	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
   136	
   137	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
   138	    }
   139	}
