   140	//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
   141	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
   142	use super::*;
   143	const LIMIT:usize=512;
   144	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
   145	pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}
   146	
   147	pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
   148	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
   149	    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
   150	    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
   151	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
   152	        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
   153	        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
   154	    }}
   155	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
   156	    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
   157	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
   158	    out.into_iter().collect()
   159	}
   160	fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
   161	    let mut out=viewers(bound);
   162	    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
   163	        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
   164	    }}}
   165	    out
   166	}
   167	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
   168	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
   169	        Some(match k{
   170	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
   171	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
   172	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
   173	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
   174	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
   175	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
   176	        })
   177	    }
   178	    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
   179	    for (name,k) in args{
   180	        let Some(vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
   181	        let mut next=Vec::new();
   182	        for n in 0..vals.len() {for c in &cases{
   183	            let mut case=c.clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[n%vals.len()].clone());
   184	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{break;}
   185	        }if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break;}}
   186	        cases=next;
   187	    }cases
   188	}
   189	fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
   190	    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
   191	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)>=committed(steps)){return;}
   192	    out.violations.retain(|v|v.invariant_site!=inv.site.to_string()||v.viewer!=viewer||v.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace);
   193	    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});
   194	}
   195	fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value)->bool{
   196	    use crate::types::Ty;
   197	    match ty{
   198	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key),
   199	        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key),
   200	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|matches!(r,Value::Row(_))&&r.field("id").ok()==Some(key))),
   201	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key))),
   202	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key))),
   203	        _=>false,
   204	    }
   205	}
   206	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   207	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c=="id")){
   208	        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],None),p)==Ok(true));
   209	        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
   210	        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}
   211	
   212	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
   213	            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
   214	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()>=committed){continue;}
   215	            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
   216	            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
   217	            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
   218	            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
   219	            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
   220	                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,rows,actors,out);
   221	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
   222	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   223	                    out.attempted+=1;
   224	                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&q.complete&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key){
   225	                        violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
   226	                        if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query returned a compiler-typed row of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
   227	                        continue 'actors;
   228	                    }
   229	                }
   230	            }
   231	            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
   232	            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   233	            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
   234	            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   235	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   236	            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
   237	                let p=&bp.program;
   238	                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
   239	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
   240	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   241	                    out.attempted+=2;
   242	                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
   243	                    if a==b{continue;}
   244	                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
   245	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
   246	                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
   247	                    continue 'actors;
   248	                }
   249	            }
   250	        }}
   251	    }
   252	}
   253	fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
   254	    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
   255	}
   256	fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   257	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   258	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   259	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   260	            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   261	            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
   262	            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
   263	            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
   264	                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
   265	            }
   266	        }
   267	    }
   268	}
   269	fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   270	    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
   271	    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
   272	    for inv in &bound.invariants{
   273	        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
   274	        let judged;
   275	        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
   276	            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
   277	            judged=stable;&judged
   278	        };
   279	        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
   280	        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   281	        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
   282	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
   283	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
   284	            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],old)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   285	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   286	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
   287	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
   288	            }
   289	        }
   290	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   291	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],r));
   292	            if old==Some(row){continue;}
   293	            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
   294	            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
   295	            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
   296	            if inv.target=="always" {
   297	                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
   298	            }else{
   299	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   300	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
   301	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
   302	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
   303	                }
   304	            }
   305	        }
   306	    }
   307	}
   308	fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   309	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
   310	    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   311	    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return out;};
   312	    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
   313	        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
   314	        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
   315	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
   316	        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
   317	            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
   318	            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
   319	            let mut targets=Vec::new();
   320	            for case in cases{
   321	                let mut args=case.clone();
   322	                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
   323	                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
   324	                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
   325	                }}
   326	                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
   327	            }
   328	            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
   329	            for actor in actors{
   330	                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
   331	                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
   332	                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
   333	                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   334	            }
   335	            groups.push(row_cases);
   336	        }
   337	    }
   338	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
   339	    out
   340	}
   341	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   342	    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
   343	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   344	    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
   345	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   346	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   347	    let probes=Vec::new();
   348	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   349	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
   350	        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
   351	        let mut group=role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture);
   352	        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
   353	        for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
   354	            if cases.is_empty(){break;}
   355	            group.push((&bp.program,actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()].clone(),cases[n%cases.len()].clone()));
   356	        }}
   357	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   358	    }
   359	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
   360	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
   361	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   362	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   363	    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
   364	    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
   365	    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
   366	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
   367	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   368	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   369	        for i in indices{
   370	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   371	            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
   372	            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
   373	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   374	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
   375	                Ok(result)=>{
   376	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   377	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   378	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   379	                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   380	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   381	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   382	            }
   383	        }
   384	    }
   385	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   386	}

     1	//! Finite three-valued implication, deliberately independent of database facts.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — impossible valuations overapproximate all snapshots.
     3	use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr as R, RuleTerm as T};
     4	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     5	
     6	pub const MAX_ATOMS: usize = 10;
     7	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
     8	pub enum Truth { Yes, No, Unknown }
     9	impl Truth {
    10	    fn not(self) -> Self { match self { Self::Yes => Self::No, Self::No => Self::Yes, Self::Unknown => Self::Unknown } }
    11	    fn combine(self, other: Self, and: bool) -> Self {
    12	        let decisive = if and { Self::No } else { Self::Yes };
    13	        if self == decisive || other == decisive { decisive }
    14	        else if self == Self::Unknown || other == Self::Unknown { Self::Unknown }
    15	        else { decisive.not() }
    16	    }
    17	}
    18	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
    19	enum Formula { Constant(Truth), Atom(usize), Not(Box<Self>), And(Vec<Self>), Or(Vec<Self>) }
    20	impl Formula {
    21	    fn eval(&self, values: &[Truth]) -> Truth {
    22	        match self {
    23	            Self::Constant(v) => *v, Self::Atom(n) => values[*n], Self::Not(x) => x.eval(values).not(),
    24	            Self::And(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::Yes, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), true)),
    25	            Self::Or(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::No, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), false)),
    26	        }
    27	    }
    28	}
    29	fn term(t: &T) -> T { match t { T::NextField(f) => T::Field(f.clone()), other => other.clone() } }
    30	fn atom(rule: &R) -> String {
    31	    let mut r = rule.clone();
    32	    match &mut r {
    33	        R::Eq(a,b) | R::Compare(_,a,b) => { *a=term(a); *b=term(b); }
    34	        R::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | R::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
    35	            for t in key { *t=term(t); }
    36	            for (_,t) in equalities.iter_mut() { *t=term(t); }
    37	            equalities.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    38	        }
    39	        _ => {}
    40	    }
    41	    if let R::Eq(a,b)=&mut r && serde_json::to_string(a).unwrap()>serde_json::to_string(b).unwrap() { std::mem::swap(a,b); }
    42	    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(&r).unwrap();
    43	    // Provenance controls planning, not rule evaluation. Remove only this
    44	    // leaf's metadata, never a similarly named literal-object field.
    45	    for name in ["Exists","Created"] { if let Some(body)=json.get_mut(name).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object_mut) { body.remove("provenance"); } }
    46	    json.to_string()
    47	}
    48	fn formula(rule: &R, atoms: &mut Vec<String>, nodes: &mut usize) -> Result<Formula, String> {
    49	    *nodes+=1; if *nodes>1024 { return Err("formula exceeds 1024 nodes".into()); }
    50	    Ok(match rule {
    51	        R::Allow | R::Public {..} => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes), R::Deny => Formula::Constant(Truth::No),
    52	        R::And(xs) => Formula::And(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
    53	        R::Or(xs) => Formula::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
    54	        R::Not(x) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(x,atoms,nodes)?)),
    55	        R::Ne(a,b) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(&R::Eq(a.clone(),b.clone()),atoms,nodes)?)),
    56	        R::Eq(T::Literal(a),T::Literal(b)) => Formula::Constant(if a==b {Truth::Yes}else{Truth::No}),
    57	        R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Viewer) => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes),
    58	        other => { let key=atom(other); let n=atoms.iter().position(|a| a==&key).unwrap_or_else(|| {atoms.push(key);atoms.len()-1});
    59	            if atoms.len()>MAX_ATOMS {return Err(format!("more than {MAX_ATOMS} independent predicates"));} Formula::Atom(n) }
    60	    })
    61	}
    62	/// `Ok(true)` is a proof; `Ok(false)` is only an abstract countermodel.
    63	/// Neither limit exhaustion nor a countermodel is a concrete refutation.
    64	pub fn implies(admission: &R, policy: &R) -> Result<bool,String> {
    65	    let (mut atoms,mut nodes)=(Vec::new(),0);
    66	    let a=formula(admission,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    67	    let p=formula(policy,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    68	    let mut values=vec![Truth::Unknown;atoms.len()];
    69	    for mut n in 0..3usize.pow(atoms.len() as u32) {
    70	        for v in &mut values { *v=match n%3 {0=>Truth::Yes,1=>Truth::No,_=>Truth::Unknown}; n/=3; }
    71	        if a.eval(&values)==Truth::Yes && p.eval(&values)!=Truth::Yes { return Ok(false); }
    72	    }
    73	    Ok(true)
    74	}
    75	pub fn dependencies(rule: &R) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    76	    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    77	    match rule {
    78	        R::Exists{table,key,equalities,..}|R::Created{table,key,equalities,..} => {out.insert(table.clone());for term in key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)){for (table,_) in term.references(){out.insert(table.into());}}}
    79	        R::And(xs)|R::Or(xs) => for x in xs {out.extend(dependencies(x));}, R::Not(x) => out.extend(dependencies(x)),
    80	        R::Eq(a,b)|R::Ne(a,b)|R::Compare(_,a,b) => for t in [a,b] { for (table,_) in t.references() {out.insert(table.into());} },
    81	        _=>{}
    82	    }
    83	    out
    84	}

     1	//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
     5	type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
     6	fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
     7	    let mut expr=expr.clone();
     8	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
     9	        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
    10	        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    11	    });expr
    12	}
    13	fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    14	    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    15	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
    16	        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
    17	        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
    18	            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
    19	            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
    20	            match &f.body{
    21	                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
    22	                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
    23	            }
    24	        },_=>{}
    25	    });
    26	}
    27	// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
    28	// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
    29	fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    30	    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    31	    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    32	    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    33	    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    34	    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    35	    let mut out=Vec::new();
    36	    for s in body{match s{
    37	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
    38	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
    39	        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
    40	        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    41	    }}out
    42	}
    43	fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    44	    for s in body{match s{
    45	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
    46	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
    47	            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
    48	            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
    49	        },
    50	        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
    51	        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
    52	        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{
    53	            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
    54	            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
    55	            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
    56	        },
    57	        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
    58	        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    59	    }}
    60	}
    61	pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    62	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    63	    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    64	    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
    65	}

    31	    },

     1	//! Conservative transitive write closure for pre-write policy snapshots.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#127-stable-judgments-and-direct-payload-witnesses — implicit writes cannot establish pre-write intent.
     3	use super::*;
     4	pub(super) fn implicit(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->BTreeSet<String>{
     5	    let mut affected=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
     6	    let mut implicit=BTreeSet::new();
     7	    loop{
     8	        let before=affected.len();
     9	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if affected.contains(&m.source)||m.row_key.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|affected.contains(&r.table)){
    10	            if bound.schema.table(&m.target).is_some(){implicit.insert(m.target.clone());affected.insert(m.target.clone());}
    11	            if let Some(owner)=&m.private{implicit.insert(owner.table.clone());affected.insert(owner.table.clone());}
    12	        }}
    13	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{
    14	            if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|edge.live.is_some()&&affected.contains(&edge.parent)){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
    15	            for (column,action) in &t.on_delete{
    16	            if action.writes()&&let Some(C::Ref{table})=t.columns.get(column).map(C::required)&&affected.contains(table){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
    17	        }}
    18	        if before==affected.len(){break;}
    19	    }implicit
    20	}
    21	
    22	pub(super) fn dependencies(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{
    23	    let mut deps=kernel::dependencies(rule);
    24	    loop{let before=deps.len();for name in deps.clone(){if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&name){deps.extend(kernel::dependencies(&t.rules.read));if let Some(edge)=&t.through{deps.insert(edge.parent.clone());}}}if before==deps.len(){return deps;}}
    25	}
    26	/// A stable necessary condition may refute an intent even when another
    27	/// conjunct changes. Dropping a requirement never proves the full policy.
    28	pub(super) fn necessary(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,inv:&Invariant)->Option<Invariant>{
    29	    let policy=inv.policy.as_ref()?;let implicit=implicit(bound,p);
    30	    if implicit.contains(&inv.table){return None;}
    31	    let mut changed=implicit;
    32	    changed.extend(ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table).map(|(t,_)|t));
    33	    fn weak(bound:&Bound,r:&RuleExpr,changed:&BTreeSet<String>)->RuleExpr{
    34	        match r{
    35	            RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
    36	            RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
    37	            _ if dependencies(bound,r).iter().any(|d|changed.contains(d))=>RuleExpr::Allow,
    38	            _=>r.clone(),
    39	        }
    40	    }
    41	    let weaker=weak(bound,policy,&changed);
    42	    if kernel::implies(policy,&weaker)!=Ok(true){return None;}
    43	    let mut judged=inv.clone();judged.policy=Some(weaker);Some(judged)
    44	}

     8	pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{

    28	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    29	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
    30	        Some(match k{
    31	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
    32	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
    33	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
    34	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
    35	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
    36	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    37	        })
    38	    }

    41	        let Some(vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
    42	        let mut next=Vec::new();
    43	        for n in 0..vals.len() {for c in &cases{
    44	            let mut case=c.clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[n%vals.len()].clone());
    45	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{break;}
    46	        }if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break;}}
    47	        cases=next;
    48	    }cases
    49	}
    50	fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
    51	    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    52	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)>=committed(steps)){return;}

    55	}
    56	fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value)->bool{
    57	    use crate::types::Ty;
    58	    match ty{
    59	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key),
    60	        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key),
    61	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|matches!(r,Value::Row(_))&&r.field("id").ok()==Some(key))),
    62	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key))),
    63	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key))),
    64	        _=>false,
    65	    }
    66	}

    83	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
    84	                    out.attempted+=1;
    85	                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&q.complete&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key){
    86	                        violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
    87	                        if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query returned a compiler-typed row of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
    88	                        continue 'actors;
    89	                    }
    90	                }
    91	            }
    92	            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
    93	            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
    94	            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
    95	            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
    96	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
    97	            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
    98	                let p=&bp.program;
    99	                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
   100	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
   101	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   102	                    out.attempted+=2;
   103	                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
   104	                    if a==b{continue;}
   105	                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
   106	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
   107	                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
   108	                    continue 'actors;
   109	                }
   110	            }
   111	        }}
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
   115	    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
   116	}
   117	fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   118	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   119	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   120	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   121	            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   122	            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
   123	            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
   124	            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
   125	                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
   126	            }
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	}
   130	fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   131	    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
   132	    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
   133	    for inv in &bound.invariants{
   134	        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
   135	        let judged;
   136	        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
   137	            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
   138	            judged=stable;&judged
   139	        };
   140	        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
   141	        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   142	        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
   143	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
   144	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
   145	            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],old)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   146	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   147	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
   148	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
   149	            }
   150	        }

     9	fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    10	    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    11	    match rule{
    12	        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
    13	            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    14	            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
    15	            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}
    16	        },
    17	        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    18	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
    19	        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
    20	        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
    21	        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
    22	        _=>rule.clone(),
    23	    }

    25	pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],_dynamic:bool)->Option<String>{
    26	    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    27	    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    28	    let gates=match op{ast::WriteOp::Insert=>vec![&table.rules.insert],ast::WriteOp::Update=>vec![&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Upsert=>vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Delete=>return None};
    29	    let roles=match table.columns.get(column)?.required(){snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}=>variants.clone(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text{..}=>{
    30	        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
    31	        fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{

    33	            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
    34	        }}

    36	    },_=>return None};
    37	    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&i.operation.is_none()&&matches!(i.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"write")).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    38	    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
    39	        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(_,v)|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
    40	        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    41	    }}
    42	    let mut all_branches=gates.iter().flat_map(|r|r.disjuncts()).filter(|b|b.service_grant().is_none()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    43	    if op!=ast::WriteOp::Insert&&let Some(owner)=table.written_by.get(column){all_branches.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));}
    44	    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(RuleExpr::Or(all_branches)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    45	    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    46	    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    47	    for target in roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)){
    48	        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
    49	            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
    50	            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
    51	            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
    52	            if !admits{continue;}

    54	            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
    55	            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
    56	            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
    57	            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}

    61	            }}

    45	fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    46	    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    47	    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    48	    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
    49	        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    50	    });}
    51	    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    52	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    53	    let mut enum_case=variant;
    54	    for name in names {
    55	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
    56	        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
    57	        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
    58	        let mut principal_index=0usize;
    59	        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
    60	            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
    61	            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>(3+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
    62	            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
    63	                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
    64	            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
    65	        }
    66	        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
    67	            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
    68	        }}}
    69	        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
    70	            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
    71	        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
    72	        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
    73	        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
    74	        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
    75	        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    76	    }
    77	    Ok(out)
    78	}
