    91	    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
    92	}
    93	fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{
    94	
    95	    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};
    96	
    97	    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    98	    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
    99	    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
   100	}
   101	fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
   102	    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
   103	    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
   104	    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
   105	    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
   106	        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
   107	    }
   108	}
   109	fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
   110	fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
   111	    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
   112	}
   113	fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   114	    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
   115	}
   116	fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
   117	    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
   118	    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   119	    fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
   120	        match (kind,value) {
   121	            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
   122	            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
   123	            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
   124	            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
   125	            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
   129	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
   130	        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
   131	        (name.clone(),clean)
   132	    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
   133	    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
   134	        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
   135	            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
   136	            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
   137	        }
   138	    }}}
   139	    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
   140	    let actual=readback(&engine)?;
   141	    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
   142	        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   143	        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
   144	            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
   145	            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
   146	        }}
   147	    }}
   148	    Ok(engine)
   149	}
   150	fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
   151	    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
   152	    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
   153	    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
   154	    let probe=match word{
   155	        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
   156	        "insert"=>{seeds.remove(&table);program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Literal(row.clone())},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])},
   157	        "delete"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Delete{table,key:Expr::Literal(key)},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]),
   158	        "update"=>{
   159	            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
   160	            let changed=definition.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|n.as_str()!="id"&&!definition.engine_field(n))
   161	                .filter(|(n,_)|inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|c==*n))
   162	                .filter(|(n,_)|!definition.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(n))))
   163	                .find_map(|(n,k)| {let old=row.field(n).ok()?;let next=match (k.required(),old){
   164	                    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
   165	                    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|v|Some(Value::String((*v).clone()))!=Some(old.clone()))?.clone()),
   166	                    (C::Text{max},Value::String(v)) if v.chars().count()<*max as usize=>Value::String(format!("{v}x")),
   167	                    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(v))=>Value::Int(v+1),_=>return None};
   168	                    Some((n.clone(),next))});
   169	            let Some((column,value))=changed else{return Err("no valid changed mutable-column sample".into());};
   170	            program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Update{table,key:Expr::Literal(key),patch:Expr::Literal(Value::Map([(column,value)].into_iter().collect()))},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])
   171	        },_=>return Err("row integrity/transition challenge is not an operation admission test".into()),
   172	    };
   173	    let mut engine=engine(bound,&seeds,probe)?;
   174	    if word=="read"{return engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string());}
   175	    let outcome=engine.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   176	    if !matches!(outcome.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){return Ok((false,row.clone()));}
   177	    let next=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   178	    if word=="delete"{return if next.is_none(){Ok((true,row.clone()))}else{Err("delete returned sent without removing the target".into())};}
   179	    let next=next.ok_or("sent write has no committed target image")?;
   180	    if word=="update" {
   181	        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   182	        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
   183	            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
   184	        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
   185	    }
   186	    Ok((true,next))
   187	}
   188	fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}
   189	
   190	fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
   191	    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
   192	    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
   193	    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
   194	    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
   195	    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
   196	    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   197	    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
   198	    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
   199	        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   200	        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
   201	    });
   202	    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
   203	        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
   204	    }Ok(())
   205	}
   206	
   207	/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
   208	/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
   209	pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
   210	/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
   211	/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
   212	pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{
   213	    let mut out=Campaign::default();
   214	    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
   215	    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
   216	    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
   217	    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
   218	        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
   219	            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
   220	                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
   221	        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   222	    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
   223	    for inv in &bound.invariants {
   224	        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
   225	        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
   226	        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
   227	            let relevant=ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table&&(inv.target=="write"||inv.target==*kind||*kind=="upsert"));
   228	            if relevant{let mut op=inv.clone();op.operation=Some(p.program.name.clone());assertions.push(op);}
   229	        }
   230	    }
   231	    for inv in &assertions{
   232	        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
   233	        if sites.is_some_and(|sites|!sites.contains(&inv.site.to_string())){continue;}
   234	        if inv.operation.is_none()&&discharged.contains(&inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
   235	        if let Err(why)=operation_preflight(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}
   236	        let mut found=false;
   237	        let combinations=bound.schema.tables.values().flat_map(|t|t.columns.values()).filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).fold(1usize,usize::saturating_mul).max(24);
   238	        if combinations>512{out.unsupported.insert("schema enum Cartesian matrix exceeds 512 samples; remaining tuples are not covered".into());}
   239	        for sample in 0..=combinations.min(512) {
   240	            let variant=sample.saturating_sub(1);
   241	            let mut rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   242	            // Try explicit policy field literals before the unchanged enum
   243	            // matrix. A value such as status='cancelled' need not wait behind
   244	            // hundreds of unrelated enum tuples. This is only a candidate;
   245	            // ordinary validation and engine replay still decide refutation.
   246	            if sample==0 {
   247	                fn pin(rule:&RuleExpr,row:&mut Value)->bool{match rule{
   248	                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|
   249	                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))=>{
   250	                        if let Value::Row(fields)=row&&fields.contains_key(f){fields.insert(f.clone(),v.clone());true}else{false}
   251	                    },
   252	                    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().fold(false,|any,r|pin(r,row)||any),
   253	                    RuleExpr::Not(r)=>pin(r,row),_=>false,
   254	                }}
   255	                if !inv.policy.as_ref().zip(rows.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first_mut())).is_some_and(|(p,row)|pin(p,row)){continue;}
   256	            }
   257	            let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"security read".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   258	            let rows=match readback(&seeded){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   259	            let Some(row)=rows.get(&inv.table).and_then(|r|r.first())else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no generated ordinary row",inv.table));break;};
   260	            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"]{
   261	                if inv.target=="always" {
   262	                    match policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row){
   263	                        Ok(false)=>{out.attempted+=1;out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::TrustedSeedOnly,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:"$security.seed_at".into(),effect:"always".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:"trusted seed ingress; all typed and final-exists constraints rechecked".into(),scope:"actual seed_at commit/read-back; ordinary application reachability is not claimed".into()});found=true;},
   264	                        Ok(true)=>{out.attempted+=1;},Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);}
   265	                    }if found{break;}continue;
   266	                }
   267	                if let Some(operation)=&inv.operation {
   268	                    let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==operation)else{continue;};
   269	                    let permitted=policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row);
   270	                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.program.args,variant)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: arguments need fixture assets or unsupported samples"));continue;};
   271	                    out.attempted+=1;
   272	                    let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   273	                    let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut seeded,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}"));continue;}};
   274	                    match seeded.mutate(operation,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)) {
   275	                        Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})=>{
   276	                            let current=readback(&seeded).unwrap_or_default();
   277	                            let changed=current.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|!rows.get(&inv.table).is_some_and(|old|old.contains(r))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   278	                            for next in changed {
   279	                                let old=rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==next.field("id").ok());
   280	                                // An upsert must be attributed to its executed arm;
   281	                                // a column contract cannot be refuted by editing a sibling.
   282	                                if inv.target=="update"&&old.is_none() || inv.target=="insert"&&old.is_some() || inv.target=="delete" {continue;}
   283	                                if let Some(column)=&inv.column && old.is_some_and(|old|old.field(column).ok()==next.field(column).ok()){continue;}
   284	                                if permitted==Ok(false){out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:operation.clone(),effect:if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"}.into(),row:next.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:format!("authored operation {operation}"),scope:"actual authored mutation committed a changed image from a schema-valid seeded snapshot".into()});found=true;break;}
   285	                            }
   286	                        },_=>out.refused+=1,
   287	                    }
   288	                    if found{break;}continue;
   289	                }
   290	                for word in super::effects(inv){
   291	                    let mut admission_rows=rows.clone();if word=="insert"{admission_rows.remove(&inv.table);}
   292	                    let judged=if word=="read"{policy_in(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,Some(row),row,&mut seeded)}else{policy(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,(word!="insert").then_some(row),row)};
   293	                    let allowed=match judged{Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   294	                    if allowed{continue;}out.attempted+=1;
   295	                    let replayed=if word=="read" {seeded.query("$security.seed",Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string())}else{replay(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,word,row)};
   296	                    match replayed{
   297	                        Ok((false,_))=>out.refused+=1,
   298	                        Ok((true,next))=>{
   299	                            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
   300	                            if word=="update"{let changes=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.keys().filter(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()).filter_map(|c|next.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect();args.insert("$syntheticPatch".into(),Value::Map(changes));}
   301	                            out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args,viewer:viewer.into(),operation:format!("$security.{}.{}",inv.table,word),effect:word.into(),row:next,old:(word=="update"||word=="delete").then(||row.clone()),supporting_rows:admission_rows.clone(),rule_path:bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).quarry(),scope:if word=="update"{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; old-image policy false at an actual changed update; committed next image and patch shown; application reachability is not claimed".into()}else{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; synthetic engine table operation; application reachability is not claimed".into()}});found=true;break;},
   302	                        Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: {e}",inv.table,word));},
   303	                    }
   304	                }
   305	                if found{break;}
   306	            }
   307	            if found{break;}
   308	        }
   309	    }
   310	    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
   311	    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
   312	    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
   313	    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
   314	    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
   315	}
   316	pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());for c in &campaign.violations{for v in &mut report.invariants{if v.invariant.site.to_string()==c.invariant_site{
   317	    if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|old.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)&&c.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{continue;}
   318	    v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; finite test evidence, not a reachability theorem".into());
   319	}}}}
   320	
   321	fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
   322	    use ir::ArgType as A;
   323	    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
   324	        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
   325	        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),A::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),_=>return None,
   326	    })}
   327	    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   328	}
   329	
   330	fn materialize_arguments(engine:&mut Engine,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,values:&BTreeMap<String,Value>,viewer:&str)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,String>{
   331	    fn one(e:&mut Engine,k:&ir::ArgType,v:&Value,viewer:&str)->Result<Value,String>{Ok(match (k,v){
   332	        (ir::ArgType::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>one(e,inner,v,viewer)?,
   333	        (ir::ArgType::List{of:Some(inner),..}|ir::ArgType::ListRange{of:Some(inner),..},Value::Array(xs))=>Value::Array(xs.iter().map(|v|one(e,inner,v,viewer)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
   334	        (ir::ArgType::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>e.store_asset(viewer,&PNG,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.column_value(),
   335	        (ir::ArgType::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(e,viewer,types)?,
   336	        _=>v.clone(),
   337	    })}
   338	    values.iter().map(|(n,v)|one(engine,&args[n],v,viewer).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   339	}
   340	
   341	/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
   342	/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
   343	/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
   344	pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   345	    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   346	    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
   347	}
   348	pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   349	    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
   350	    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   351	    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   352	    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
   353	    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
   354	    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
   355	    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
   356	    let quota=(8192/bound.programs.len().max(1)).clamp(16,512);
   357	    for p in &bound.programs{
   358	        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
   359	        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
   360	        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
   361	        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
   362	        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program.args,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
   363	        let mut exercised=0;
   364	        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
   365	            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
   366	            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
   367	            for viewer in &viewers{
   368	                if exercised==quota{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
   369	                exercised+=1;
   370	                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   371	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   372	                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   373	                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
   374	                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
   375	                }else{
   376	                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
   377	                }
   378	            }
   379	        }
   380	    }
   381	}
   382	
   383	fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   384	    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
   385	    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
   386	}

     1	//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
     5	const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
     6	fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
     7	fn role_test(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..} if key.iter().any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&equalities.iter().any(|(_,t)|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))))}
     8	fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
     9	fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
    10	fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    11	    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    12	    let roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    13	    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    14	    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    15	    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
    16	        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
    17	        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
    18	        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    19	    }
    20	    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
    21	        match value{
    22	            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
    23	                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
    24	                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
    25	                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
    26	                }}
    27	                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
    28	                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
    29	                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
    30	                }}
    31	                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
    32	            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
    33	        }
    34	    }
    35	    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
    36	        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
    37	        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
    38	        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
    39	        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    40	    })
    41	}
    42	fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    43	    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster))){return true;}
    44	    let mut recipients=reader_columns(branch);
    45	    if let Some(grant)=grant&&let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(grant){
    46	        for leaf in rule_parts(branch){if let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=leaf&&table==grant{
    47	            if let Some(idx)=g.index(index){for (part,key) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if matches!(key,RuleTerm::Viewer)&&let Some(c)=part.column(){recipients.insert(c.into());}}}
    48	            for (c,key) in equalities{if matches!(key,RuleTerm::Viewer){recipients.insert(c.clone());}}
    49	        }}
    50	        if recipients.is_empty(){recipients.extend(g.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).map(|(c,_)|c.clone()));}
    51	    }
    52	    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(roster)else{return false;};
    53	    let normal=|s:&str|s.chars().filter(|c|c.is_alphabetic()).flat_map(char::to_lowercase).collect::<String>();
    54	    super::privilege::role_columns(bound,roster).iter().any(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}) if variants.iter().any(|r|{let r=normal(r);recipients.iter().any(|c|{let c=normal(c);c.len()>2&&r.len()>2&&(c.ends_with(&r)||r.ends_with(&c))})})))
    55	}
    56	pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    57	    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    58	    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
    59	        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
    60	        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
    61	            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
    62	            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
    63	        }
    64	        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
    65	            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
    66	            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
    67	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
    68	            }
    69	        }
    70	        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
    71	            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
    72	                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
    73	                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
    74	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
    75	            }
    76	        }
    77	        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
    78	          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
    79	            if role_test(branch){continue;}
    80	            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
    81	                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    82	                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(&g.rules.insert)||role_test(&g.rules.update)){
    83	                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
    84	                }
    85	            }}
    86	            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&[&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).any(|r|mutable_role(bound,&r)&&recipient_role(bound,name,branch,None,&r,effect))&&(role_test(&t.rules.insert)||role_test(&t.rules.update)) {
    87	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: stored principal `{}` grants authority after a mutable role changes",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as stored attribution; guide security");
    88	            }
    89	          }
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    93	    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    94	    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    95	    let _=exposures;
    96	    notes
    97	}
    98	pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    99	    let mut out=Vec::new();
   100	    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,source)|crate::parse_document(file,source).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   101	    let catalog=super::super::catalog::Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
   102	    for doc in documents{
   103	        for p in &doc.programs {
   104	            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
   105	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   106	            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
   107	            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
   108	                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
   109	                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
   110	                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
   111	                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
   112	                    });
   113	                }
   114	            }
   115	            for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
   116	                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=&write else{continue;};
   117	                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{continue;};
   118	                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
   119	                for column in &role_columns {if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)||matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)&&t.defaults.contains_key(column))&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
   120	                    let mut targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   121	                    if !fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&let Some(snapback4_core::Value::String(role))=t.defaults.get(column).map(|d|d.value(NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS,"$advisory")){targets.push(role);}
   122	                    let chosen=fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column);
   123	                    let dynamic=chosen.is_none()||chosen.is_some_and(|f|!matches!(f.value,Some(Expr::Literal{..})));
   124	                    if let Some(field)=chosen {
   125	                        if let Some(Expr::Name{name:argument,..})=&field.value&&let Some(arg)=p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==*argument)&&let ast::TypeKind::Enum{variants}= &arg.type_.kind {targets=variants.clone();}
   126	                    }
   127	                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
   128	                    let effective_op=if chosen.is_none()&&*op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op};
   129	                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&targets,effective_op,&requirements,dynamic)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$privilege".into())) {
   130	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
   131	                    }
   132	                }}
   133	            }
   134	            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
   135	                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
   136	                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
   137	                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
   138	                for f in fields.iter(){
   139	                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
   140	                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
   141	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
   142	                    }
   143	
   144	                }
   145	            });
   146	            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
   147	            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
   148	            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
   149	                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
   150	                    *absent=None;
   151	                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
   152	                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
   153	                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
   154	                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
   155	                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
   156	                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
   157	                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
   158	                        }
   159	                    }
   160	                },
   161	                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
   162	                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
   163	                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
   164	            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
   165	            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
   166	            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
   167	                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
   168	                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
   169	                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
   170	                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
   171	                }}}
   172	                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
   173	                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
   174	                if !clock{continue;}
   175	                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
   176	                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
   177	                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
   178	                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
   179	                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
   180	                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
   181	                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
   182	                    }}
   183	                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
   184	                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
   185	                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
   186	                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
   187	                    });}
   188	                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
   189	                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
   190	                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
   191	                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
   192	                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
   193	                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
   194	                }}
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	    }
   198	    out
   199	}

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.

     4	fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
     5	    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
     6	    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
     7	    pairs
     8	}
     9	fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    10	    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    11	    match rule{
    12	        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
    13	            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    14	            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
    15	            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}
    16	        },
    17	        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    18	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
    19	        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
    20	        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
    21	        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
    22	        _=>rule.clone(),
    23	    }
    24	}
    25	pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],_dynamic:bool)->Option<String>{
    26	    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    27	    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    28	    let gates=match op{ast::WriteOp::Insert=>vec![&table.rules.insert],ast::WriteOp::Update=>vec![&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Upsert=>vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Delete=>return None};
    29	    let roles=match table.columns.get(column)?.required(){snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}=>variants.clone(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text{..}=>{
    30	        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
    31	        fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
    32	            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==roster=>for (c,t) in &probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities){if c==column&&let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{roles.insert(s.clone());}},
    33	            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
    34	        }}
    35	        for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,roster,column,&mut roles);}}roles.into_iter().collect()
    36	    },_=>return None};
    37	    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&i.operation.is_none()&&matches!(i.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"write")).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    38	    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
    39	        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(_,v)|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
    40	        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    41	    }}
    42	    let mut all_branches=gates.iter().flat_map(|r|r.disjuncts()).filter(|b|b.service_grant().is_none()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    43	    if op!=ast::WriteOp::Insert&&let Some(owner)=table.written_by.get(column){all_branches.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));}
    44	    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(RuleExpr::Or(all_branches)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    45	    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    46	    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    47	    for target in roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)){
    48	        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
    49	            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
    50	            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
    51	            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
    52	            if !admits{continue;}
    53	            let violates_intent=intents.iter().any(|p|kernel::implies(&admitted,&substitute(bound,p,roster,column,caller,Some(target)))==Ok(false));
    54	            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
    55	            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
    56	            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
    57	            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
    58	            for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)]{
    59	                let granted=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,caller,None);
    60	                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
    61	            }}
    62	        }
    63	    }None
    64	}

    66	pub(super) fn role_columns(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    67	    let mut columns=BTreeSet::new();
    68	    if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table){for c in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(c){columns.insert(c.into());}}}
    69	    fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,table:&str,columns:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
    70	        RuleExpr::Exists{table:t,index,key,equalities,..} if t==table&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>for (c,v) in &probe_pairs(bound,t,index,key,equalities){if matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))){columns.insert(c.clone());}},
    71	        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,table,columns)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,table,columns),_=>{}
    72	    }}
    73	    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut columns);}}
    74	    columns
    75	}

   210	/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
   211	/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
   212	pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{
   213	    let mut out=Campaign::default();
   214	    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
   215	    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
   216	    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
   217	    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()

   219	            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")

   221	        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();

   224	        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
   225	        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
   226	        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}

   316	pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());for c in &campaign.violations{for v in &mut report.invariants{if v.invariant.site.to_string()==c.invariant_site{
   317	    if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|old.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)&&c.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{continue;}
   318	    v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; finite test evidence, not a reachability theorem".into());
   319	}}}}

   321	fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
   322	    use ir::ArgType as A;
   323	    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
   324	        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
   325	        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),A::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),_=>return None,
   326	    })}
   327	    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   328	}
   329	
   330	fn materialize_arguments(engine:&mut Engine,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,values:&BTreeMap<String,Value>,viewer:&str)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,String>{
   331	    fn one(e:&mut Engine,k:&ir::ArgType,v:&Value,viewer:&str)->Result<Value,String>{Ok(match (k,v){
   332	        (ir::ArgType::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>one(e,inner,v,viewer)?,
   333	        (ir::ArgType::List{of:Some(inner),..}|ir::ArgType::ListRange{of:Some(inner),..},Value::Array(xs))=>Value::Array(xs.iter().map(|v|one(e,inner,v,viewer)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
   334	        (ir::ArgType::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>e.store_asset(viewer,&PNG,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.column_value(),
   335	        (ir::ArgType::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(e,viewer,types)?,
   336	        _=>v.clone(),
   337	    })}
   338	    values.iter().map(|(n,v)|one(engine,&args[n],v,viewer).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   339	}

   341	/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
   342	/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
   343	/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
   344	pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   345	    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   346	    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
   347	}
   348	pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   349	    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
   350	    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   351	    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   352	    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
   353	    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
   354	    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
   355	    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
   356	    let quota=(8192/bound.programs.len().max(1)).clamp(16,512);
   357	    for p in &bound.programs{
   358	        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
   359	        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
   360	        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
   361	        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
   362	        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program.args,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
   363	        let mut exercised=0;
   364	        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
   365	            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
   366	            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
   367	            for viewer in &viewers{
   368	                if exercised==quota{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
   369	                exercised+=1;
   370	                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   371	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   372	                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   373	                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
   374	                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
   375	                }else{
   376	                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
   377	                }
   378	            }
   379	        }
   380	    }
   381	}
   382	
   383	fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   384	    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
   385	    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
   386	}

     2	use super::*;
     3	impl Engine {
     4	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
     5	    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
     6	        if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()) {
     7	            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated ephemeral-hub snapshot".into()));
     8	        }
     9	        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
    10	        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
    11	        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
    12	        Ok(Self {
    13	            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
    14	            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)),
    15	            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
    16	            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,

    20	        })
    21	    }
    22	}

    24	impl Engine {
    25	    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    26	    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
    27	        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
    28	        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
    29	        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
    30	        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
    31	        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
    32	            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
    33	            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
    34	        })
    35	    }

     1	//! Isolated complete fixtures, including captured authority and asset ownership.
     2	use super::*;
     3	impl Store {
     4	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — fork committed state, never reinterpret stored rows as new seed input.
     5	    pub fn fork_memory(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
     6	        let mut fork = Self::memory()?;
     7	        {
     8	            let backup = rusqlite::backup::Backup::new(&self.conn, &mut fork.conn)?;
     9	            backup.run_to_completion(256, std::time::Duration::ZERO, None)?;
    10	        }
    11	        fork.catalog = self.catalog.clone();
    12	        fork.identity = self.identity.clone();
    13	        fork.server = self.server;
    14	        if self.assets_dir.exists() {
    15	            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fork.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
    16	            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)? {
    17	                let entry = entry.map_err(io)?;
    18	                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file() {
    19	                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset ledger contains a non-file".into()));
    20	                }
    21	                std::fs::copy(entry.path(), fork.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
    22	            }
    23	        }
    24	        Ok(fork)
    25	    }
    26	}
    27	
    28	fn io(e:std::io::Error)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string())}
    29	
    30	impl Store {
    31	    /// Independent valid worlds share verified bytes, never source row state.
    32	    pub fn empty_with_asset_ledger(&self)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
    33	        let fresh=Self::memory()?;
    34	        let ids=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    35	        for id in ids{
    36	            let mut asset=self.asset(&id)?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset disappeared".into()))?;
    37	            asset.home=None;asset.released_at=None;
    38	            fresh.record_asset(&asset)?;
    39	        }
    40	        if self.assets_dir.exists(){
    41	            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fresh.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
    42	            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)?{
    43	                let entry=entry.map_err(io)?;
    44	                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file(){return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset is not a regular file".into()));}
    45	                std::fs::copy(entry.path(),fresh.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
    46	            }
    47	        }
    48	        Ok(fresh)
    49	    }
    50	}

     1	//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
     5	use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
     6	pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
     7	    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
     8	    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
     9	        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    10	    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
    11	}
    12	fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    13	    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    14	    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    15	    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    16	    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
    17	}}
    18	pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    19	    let mut out=Vec::new();
    20	    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
    21	        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
    22	        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    23	    }out
    24	}
    25	pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    26	    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    27	    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    28	    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    29	    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    30	    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    31	    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    32	    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    33	    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    34	    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    35	    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    36	    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    37	    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    38	    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    39	    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    40	    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
    41	}
