     1	//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
     6	pub struct Observations {
     7	    pub present: serde_json::Value,
     8	    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
     9	    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
    10	}
    11	
    12	pub(super) fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    13	    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    14	    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
    15	        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
    16	            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
    17	            Ok(q)=>serde_json::to_value(&q.data).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
    18	            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
    19	        }
    20	    }else{
    21	        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
    22	            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
    23	            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
    24	                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
    25	                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
    26	                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
    27	                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
    28	                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
    29	                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
    30	                }
    31	                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
    32	            },
    33	        }
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    38	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c=="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
    39	        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
    40	        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
    41	        // Closed transitive observer set: include maintained targets, inherited
    42	        // readers and every table whose rule probes an affected population.
    43	        // An operation mentioning none of them has no logical input changed
    44	        // by this pair. This prunes experiments, never proves an obligation.
    45	        let mut observers=BTreeSet::from([inv.table.clone()]);
    46	        loop {
    47	            let before=observers.len();
    48	            for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if observers.contains(&m.source){
    49	                observers.insert(m.target.clone());
    50	                if let Some(owner)=&m.private{observers.insert(owner.table.clone());}
    51	            }}
    52	            for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables {
    53	                if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|observers.contains(&p.parent))
    54	                    || [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|d|observers.contains(d))) {
    55	                    observers.insert(name.clone());
    56	                }
    57	            }
    58	            if observers.len()==before{break;}
    59	        }
    60	        // Removing an ordinary row must not manufacture an invalid reference
    61	        // graph. More general paired deletions need a checked closure.
    62	        if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))) {
    63	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired absence needs a referentially valid world closure",inv.table));continue;
    64	        }
    65	        for variant in 0..8 {
    66	            let Ok(present)=snapshot(bound,inv,variant)else{continue;};
    67	            if present.keys().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(C::contains_asset)) {
    68	                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired fixture asset identities are not coupled",inv.table));continue;
    69	            }
    70	            let Some(row)=present.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first()).cloned()else{continue;};
    71	            let mut absent=present.clone();absent.remove(&inv.table);
    72	            // Final-exists validation is a pure judgment of this fixed seeded
    73	            // world, before operation/viewer admission. Retain its rejection
    74	            // across observers, never cache execution/resource/policy results.
    75	            let invalid=std::cell::RefCell::new([None::<String>,None]);
    76	            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"] {
    77	                for bp in &bound.programs {
    78	                    let p=&bp.program;
    79	                    if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native {continue;}
    80	                    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    81	                    let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
    82	                    if writes.iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table||deps.contains(table)||*kind!="update")
    83	                        || !writes.is_empty() && (!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty() || bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.on_delete.is_empty())) {
    84	                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired mutation lacks stable policy/implicit-write and deterministic-id reduction",p.name));continue;
    85	                    }
    86	                    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
    87	                    if !observers.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
    88	                    // Fresh generated ids and opaque viewer/work contexts are
    89	                    // not repeatable functions of these paired inputs.
    90	                    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool {match v{
    91	                        serde_json::Value::String(s)=>s=="NewId"||s=="JobAs",
    92	                        serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"Emit"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"))||m.values().any(opaque),
    93	                        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false,
    94	                    }}
    95	                    if opaque(&json){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque effects excluded from paired determinism",p.name));continue;}
    96	                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.args,variant)else{continue;};
    97	                    let execute=|seeds:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>),String>{
    98	                        let slot=usize::from(std::ptr::eq(seeds,&absent));
    99	                        if let Some(why)=&invalid.borrow()[slot]{return Err(why.clone());}
   100	                        let mut e=match engine(bound,seeds,program("$security.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){
   101	                            Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{
   102	                                if why.ends_with(": trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"){invalid.borrow_mut()[slot]=Some(why.clone());}
   103	                                return Err(why);
   104	                            },
   105	                        };
   106	                        let actual=readback(&e)?;
   107	                        // Judge the hypothetical protected row in each complete
   108	                        // committed world, before any operation can change roles.
   109	                        if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,&row,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("paired policy is not definitely false in both worlds".into());}
   110	                        observe(&mut e,p,viewer,args.clone()).map(|value|(value,actual))
   111	                    };
   112	                    let ((a,rows),(b,absent_rows))=match (execute(&present),execute(&absent)) {
   113	                        (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
   114	                        (Err(e),_)|(_,Err(e))=>{
   115	                            if !e.starts_with("paired policy is not definitely false") {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.name));}
   116	                            continue;
   117	                        },
   118	                    };
   119	                    out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
   120	                    if a==b {continue;}
   121	                    // The query order is engine-defined. Recreate both worlds
   122	                    // to decline store-scoped cursors or unstable outputs.
   123	                    if !matches!((execute(&present),execute(&absent)),(Ok((aa,_)),Ok((bb,_))) if aa==a&&bb==b){
   124	                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation is not repeatable",p.name));continue;
   125	                    }
   126	                    out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),
   127	                        operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows,
   128	                        rule_path:"paired present/absent engine executions differ while the declared privacy policy is false in both".into(),
   129	                        scope:"schema-valid seed-ingress paired worlds; authenticated logical data/refusal only; no application reachability claim".into(),
   130	                        observations:Some(Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows})});
   131	                    break;
   132	                }
   133	                if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
   134	            }
   135	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
   136	        }
   137	    }
   138	    out.unsupported.insert("paired observations exclude wire sequences/receipts, physical timing and resource/meter differences; HTTP and device histories require separate qualification".into());
   139	}

   269	    if no_cases {
   270	        eprintln!("test: no business cases executed ({skipped_business_cases} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl, or use --fixture-only when fixture loading/query probes are the intended check");
   271	    }
   272	    if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { 1 } else { 0 }
   273	}

     1	//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
     5	// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
     6	const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
     7	pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
     8	pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
     9	    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
    10	}
    11	pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    12	    match op {
    13	        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
    14	        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
    15	        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    16	    }
    17	}
    18	pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    19	    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    20	    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
    21	        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
    22	        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
    23	        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
    24	            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
    25	        } else {edge};
    26	        facts.push(edge);
    27	    }
    28	    RuleExpr::And(facts)
    29	}
    30	
    31	pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    32	    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    33	    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
    34	        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
    35	        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
    36	        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
    37	        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
    38	        _=>None,
    39	    }}
    40	    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
    41	        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
    42	            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
    43	            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
    44	        }
    45	        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
    46	        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    47	    }
    48	    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    49	    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Insert,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    50	    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
    51	}

     1	//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
     6	pub struct Obligation {
     7	    pub path: String,
     8	    pub status: Status,
     9	    pub reason: String,
    10	    pub repair: Option<String>,
    11	}
    12	impl Obligation {
    13	    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
    14	        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
    15	        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
    16	            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
    17	            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
    18	                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
    19	            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
    20	        };
    21	        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    22	    }
    23	}
    24	
    25	pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    26	    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    27	    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    28	    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    29	    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
    30	        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
    31	        Ok(super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())), policy)];
    32	    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
    33	        let admission = (|| {
    34	            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
    35	                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
    36	                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
    37	            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
    38	            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
    39	            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
    40	                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
    41	                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
    42	            }
    43	            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
    44	                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
    45	            }
    46	            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
    47	            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
    48	            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
    49	        })();
    50	        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
    51	        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
    52	            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
    53	                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
    54	                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
    55	        }
    56	    }
    57	    out
    58	}
    59	
    60	fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    61	    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
    62	        match t {
    63	            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
    64	            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
    65	            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	    Ok(match rule {
    69	        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
    70	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
    71	        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
    72	        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
    73	        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
    74	        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
    75	        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
    76	        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
    77	            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
    78	            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
    79	        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    80	    })
    81	}

     1	//! Closed enum census: a new IR statement/expression/builtin forces review.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — opaque context changes are blockers.
     3	use snapback4_core::ir::{Expr,Stmt,Builtin};
     4	pub(super) fn body(xs:&[Stmt])->bool{xs.iter().all(|s|match s{
     5	    Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Push{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value),
     6	    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>expr(condition)&&body(then_body)&&body(else_body),
     7	    Stmt::For{..}=>false,
     8	    Stmt::Require{condition,..}|Stmt::Check{condition,..}=>expr(condition),
     9	    Stmt::Insert{row,..}|Stmt::InsertIfAbsent{row,..}|Stmt::Upsert{row,..}=>expr(row),
    10	    Stmt::Update{key,patch,..}=>expr(key)&&expr(patch),
    11	    Stmt::WriteTarget{patch,condition,..}=>expr(patch)&&condition.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|body(&c.setup)&&expr(&c.value)),
    12	    Stmt::Delete{key,..}=>expr(key),Stmt::DeleteTarget{..}|Stmt::Break|Stmt::Continue=>true,
    13	    Stmt::Emit{..}=>false,
    14	})}
    15	fn expr(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{
    16	    Expr::Literal(_)|Expr::Variable(_)|Expr::Input(_)=>true,
    17	    Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base),
    18	    Expr::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().all(expr),Expr::Object(xs)=>xs.values().all(expr),
    19	    Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>expr(base)&&fields.values().all(expr),
    20	    Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>expr(left)&&expr(right),
    21	    Expr::Block{body:xs,value}=>body(xs)&&expr(value),
    22	    Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>r.key.iter().all(expr),
    23	    Expr::Scan(_)|Expr::Merge(_)=>false,
    24	    Expr::Builtin{builtin,args}=>pure(builtin)&&args.iter().all(expr),
    25	}}
    26	fn pure(b:&Builtin)->bool{match b{
    27	    Builtin::JobAs|Builtin::JobQuery{..}=>false,
    28	    Builtin::ProjectFields{..}|Builtin::ReceiptSettled{..}|Builtin::OptionalField{..}|Builtin::Calendar{..}|Builtin::Civil(_)|Builtin::Len|Builtin::Slice|Builtin::Concat|Builtin::Lower|Builtin::Trim|Builtin::Sha256|Builtin::Join|Builtin::Group{..}|Builtin::First|Builtin::Last|Builtin::At|Builtin::Min|Builtin::Max|Builtin::Extremum{..}|Builtin::ExtremumOf{..}|Builtin::Divmod{..}|Builtin::Divide{..}|Builtin::Sort{..}|Builtin::MentionTokens|Builtin::SearchPresent|Builtin::RequireUncapped|Builtin::DeterministicShare|Builtin::Count|Builtin::Sum|Builtin::CountCapped|Builtin::Connected=>true,
    29	}}

     1	//! Equal-image substitution only for engine-enforced immutable local fields.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#111-avoid-duplicate-concrete-searches — no reference or mutable-field equality is assumed.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::schema::{Table,Constraint};
     5	
     6	pub(super) fn stable(table:&Table,rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{
     7	    fn term(table:&Table,value:&mut RuleTerm){
     8	        if let RuleTerm::OldField(field)=value && (field=="id"||table.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(field)))) {
     9	            *value=RuleTerm::Field(field.clone());
    10	        }
    11	    }
    12	    fn walk(table:&Table,rule:&mut RuleExpr){match rule{
    13	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)|RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(table,a);term(table,b);},
    14	        RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}|RuleExpr::Created{key,equalities,..}=>{
    15	            for value in key.iter_mut().chain(equalities.iter_mut().map(|(_,t)|t)){term(table,value);}
    16	        },
    17	        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for rule in xs{walk(table,rule)},
    18	        RuleExpr::Not(rule)=>walk(table,rule),
    19	        RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>{},
    20	    }}
    21	    let mut out=rule.clone();walk(table,&mut out);out
    22	}
    23	pub(super) fn update(table:&Table,policy:&RuleExpr)->Result<bool,String>{
    24	    kernel::implies(&stable(table,&table.update_rule_judged()),&stable(table,&policy.old_and_next()))
    25	}

     1	//! Conservative permission map; conditional predicates are never universal grants.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — include grant writers and implicit effects.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::ir;
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
     6	pub struct Grant {pub table:String,pub predicate:String,pub writers:Vec<String>}
     7	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
     8	pub struct Exposure {pub table:String,pub columns:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub column_audiences:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub effects:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub operations:Vec<String>,pub paths:Vec<String>,pub grants:Vec<Grant>}
     9	pub(super) fn mentions(json:&serde_json::Value,table:&str)->bool {
    10	    match json {serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(k,v)| k=="table"&&v.as_str()==Some(table)||mentions(v,table)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|x|mentions(x,table)),_=>false}
    11	}
    12	pub(super) fn authored_refusal(json:&serde_json::Value)->bool {
    13	    match json {serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>fields.get("Require").is_some()||fields.values().any(authored_refusal),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(authored_refusal),_=>false}
    14	}
    15	fn writers(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->Vec<String> {
    16	    let mut out=Vec::new();let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
    17	    for effect in [Effect::Insert,Effect::Update] {out.push(format!("{effect} rule: {}",t.rules.for_effect(effect).quarry()));}
    18	    for p in &bound.programs {for (table,kind) in ir::write_tables(&p.program) {if table==name {out.push(format!("{} ({kind}, {}; {})",p.program.name,p.program.kind.word(),if p.program.kind==ir::ProgramKind::Job {"system boundary"}else{"caller rules/field authority"}));}}}
    19	    for (column,authority) in &t.written_by {out.push(format!("column {column}: owner .{} can patch under field authority",authority.owner));}
    20	    for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if m.target==name||m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|p.table==name){out.push(format!("system maintain {} from {}",m.target,m.source));}}
    21	    if !t.on_delete.is_empty(){out.push(format!("referential actions: {:?}",t.on_delete));}
    22	    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.native)||!bound.schema.action_specs.is_empty()||!bound.schema.effects.is_empty(){out.push("native/worker/external paths: opaque trusted writers (not an absence claim)".into());}
    23	    out.push("operator/system seed, import, restore and activation (trusted boundary)".into());out.sort();out.dedup();out
    24	}
    25	pub(super) fn exposures(bound:&Bound)->Vec<Exposure> {
    26	    bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name,t)| {
    27	        let columns=t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)).map(|(c,k)| {
    28	            let authority=t.written_by.get(c).map(|a|format!("; written by .{}; {}",a.owner,if a.audience{"audience readable"}else{"private to owner"})).unwrap_or_default();
    29	            (c.clone(),format!("{k}{authority}"))
    30	        }).collect();
    31	        let effects=[("read",t.rules.read.quarry()),("insert",t.rules.insert.quarry()),("update",t.update_rule_judged().quarry()),("delete",t.rules.delete.quarry())].into_iter().map(|(k,v)|(k.into(),v)).collect();
    32	        let operations=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name)).map(|p|format!("{} ({})",p.program.name,p.program.kind.word())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    33	        let mut paths=vec!["server point/list/filter/view/search/count/exists/live-refetch: ordinary ruled reads".into(),"mutation valued write images: authorized final reread; logical refusals separately unproved".into()];
    34	        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some(){paths.push(format!("live rows only: not before {:?}, expire {:?}; server clock gates delivery",t.not_before,t.expire));}
    35	        if let Some(sync)=&t.sync {paths.push(format!("sync/device envelope: {}; device access uses delivered snapshot; leave {:?}",sync.line(),t.leave));}
    36	        if t.columns.values().any(|k|k.contains_asset()){paths.push("asset read: owning-row read rule AND applicable owner-private column authority; upload placement: insert/update authority".into());}
    37	        if let Some(through)=&t.through {paths.push(format!("read through {}.{} -> {} (capture/live reduction unproved)",name,through.reference,through.parent));}
    38	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if m.source==*name||m.target==*name {paths.push(format!("derived {} -> {} (independent aggregate audience)",m.source,if m.target.starts_with("$column:"){ "maintained column" }else{&m.target}));}}
    39	        if !t.on_delete.is_empty(){paths.push(format!("incoming referential actions bypass child rules: {:?}",t.on_delete));}
    40	        let mut grants=Vec::new();let mut pending=Vec::new();let mut visited=BTreeSet::new();
    41	        for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete] {for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){pending.push((dep,r.quarry()));}}
    42	        while let Some((dep,predicate))=pending.pop(){if !visited.insert(dep.clone()){continue;}let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep)else{continue};
    43	            grants.push(Grant{table:dep.clone(),predicate,writers:writers(bound,&dep)});
    44	            for r in [&g.rules.read,&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update,&g.rules.delete]{for next in kernel::dependencies(r){pending.push((next,r.quarry()));}}
    45	        }
    46	        let column_audiences=t.columns.keys().filter(|c|!t.engine_field(c)).map(|c|(c.clone(),super::columns::admission(t,Some(c)).quarry())).collect();
    47	        Exposure{table:name.clone(),columns,column_audiences,effects,operations,paths,grants}
    48	    }).collect()
    49	}
    50	/// Role labels retain complete predicates, including relationships. Every
    51	/// entry is conditional; a roster's existence is never assumed for all users.
    52	pub(super) fn roles(exposures:&[Exposure])->BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>> {
    53	    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    54	    for x in exposures {for (effect,predicate) in &x.effects {
    55	        if predicate=="deny"{continue;}
    56	        let label=if predicate=="allow"||predicate=="public"||predicate.starts_with("public "){"every signed-in user".into()}else{format!("caller satisfying {predicate}")};
    57	        out.entry(label).or_default().push(format!("{} {effect}: {predicate}; operations: {}",x.table,x.operations.join(", ")));
    58	    }}out
    59	}

     1	//! Author-owned intent starts with a reviewable draft, never inferred policy.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#103-adoption — syntactic coverage is distinct from a proved invariant.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	pub(super) fn missing(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
     6	    bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)| {
     7	        ([&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|!matches!(r,RuleExpr::Deny)) || !t.written_by.is_empty())
     8	            && !bound.invariants.iter().any(|i| &i.table==*name)
     9	    }).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect()
    10	}
    11	
    12	/// Suggested declarations mirror enforcement, not the product specification.
    13	/// The caller must keep this outside the compiled .q source set.
    14	pub fn draft(bound:&Bound)->String{
    15	    let mut out=String::from("-- DRAFT: NOT ACCEPTED. Generated from access rules, not the app specification.\n-- Confirm or correct each policy against the spec, then copy chosen declarations\n-- into an authored snapback/*.q file. This .q.draft file is never compiled.\n-- Check current roles, grant revocation, count/existence privacy, deadlines and\n-- required evidence separately. A copied wrong rule cannot catch itself.\n-- snapback4 guide security\n\n");
    16	    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
    17	        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
    18	            if matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Deny)||covered(bound,name,effect){continue;}
    19	            let predicate=if matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Public{..}){"allow".into()}else{rule.quarry()};
    20	            if predicate.contains('$') || effect=="update" && t.update_transition {
    21	                out.push_str(&format!("-- {name}.{effect}: write intent explicitly; captured/transition rule: {predicate}\n"));
    22	            }else {out.push_str(&format!("-- Candidate from {name} {effect} rule; AUTHOR MUST CONFIRM.\ninvariant {name}.{effect} only {predicate}\n"));}
    23	        }
    24	        for (column,authority) in &t.written_by {
    25	            out.push_str(&format!("-- {name}.{column}: effective field-owner patch authority .{} = viewer bypasses the table update rule; declare column-write intent from the spec.\n",authority.owner));
    26	        }
    27	    }
    28	    out
    29	}
    30	fn covered(bound:&Bound,table:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    31	    bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==table && i.operation.is_none() && i.column.is_none()
    32	        && (i.target==effect || i.target=="write" && matches!(effect,"insert"|"update")))
    33	}
    34	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
    35	pub struct UncoveredExposure {pub table:String,pub effect:String,pub audience:String,pub row_predicate:String,pub column:Option<String>}
    36	pub(super) fn uncovered(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<UncoveredExposure>{
    37	    let mut out=exposures.iter().flat_map(|x|x.effects.iter().filter(|(effect,rule)|rule.as_str()!="deny"&&!covered(bound,&x.table,effect)).map(|(effect,predicate)|UncoveredExposure{
    38	        table:x.table.clone(),effect:effect.clone(),audience:if predicate=="allow"||predicate.starts_with("public"){"every signed-in user".into()}else{format!("caller satisfying {predicate}")},row_predicate:predicate.clone(),column:None,
    39	    })).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    40	    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (column,authority) in &t.written_by{
    41	        if covered(bound,name,"update")||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&i.operation.is_none()&&i.column.as_ref()==Some(column)&&i.target=="write"){continue;}
    42	        out.push(UncoveredExposure{table:name.clone(),effect:"update".into(),column:Some(column.clone()),audience:format!("owner .{} = viewer",authority.owner),row_predicate:format!("field-owner patch authority for {column}; table update rule may deny")});
    43	    }}out
    44	}

     1	//! Exercise actual logical lifetime reads between deadlines and cleanup ticks.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — no snapshot-only clock refutation.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
     6	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock)
     7	        && sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
     8	        let policy=inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap();
     9	        if policy.has_reference(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: temporal reference policy needs authorized per-tick engine replay",inv.table));continue;}
    10	        if !kernel::dependencies(policy).is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: clock policy with raw-probe dependencies needs a live-snapshot evaluator",inv.table));continue;}
    11	        let name="$security.temporal";
    12	        let probe=program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"temporal security".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),
    13	            key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))]);
    14	        for variant in [0,4] {
    15	            let rows=match snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,true){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
    16	            for now in [NOW,NOW+999,NOW+1000,NOW+1001,NOW+1999,NOW+2000,NOW+2001] {
    17	                // Recreate the store for each boundary; never sweep before the
    18	                // read. Unswept expiry remains a logical absence by contract.
    19	                let mut engine=match engine(bound,&rows,probe.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
    20	                let actual=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
    21	                let Some(row)=actual.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())else{continue;};
    22	                for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER] {
    23	                    out.attempted+=1;
    24	                    let Ok(q)=engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(now))else{continue;};
    25	                    if q.unavailable||!q.complete||q.data==Value::Null{continue;}
    26	                    // Scalar clock atoms are evaluated only after a real
    27	                    // engine read returned this committed row. This extra
    28	                    // field is proof-private and never enters the store.
    29	                    let mut image=row.clone();
    30	                    if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image {fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(now));}
    31	                    if matches!(rules::evaluate(policy,viewer,None,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}),Ok(false)) {
    32	                        out.admitted+=1;
    33	                        out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:name.into(),
    34	                            effect:"read".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:actual.clone(),rule_path:format!("server read at {now}, before any physical sweep; clock policy false"),
    35	                            scope:"actual engine read over schema-valid committed seed ingress at the recorded server clock; schedules are not assumed to have run".into()});
    36	                        break;
    37	                    }
    38	                }
    39	            }
    40	        }
    41	    }
    42	}

     1	//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
     6	pub struct Obligation {
     7	    pub path: String,
     8	    pub status: Status,
     9	    pub reason: String,
    10	    pub repair: Option<String>,
    11	}
    12	impl Obligation {
    13	    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
    14	        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
    15	        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
    16	            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
    17	            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
    18	                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
    19	            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
    20	        };
    21	        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    22	    }
    23	}
    24	
    25	pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    26	    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    27	    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    28	    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    29	    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
    30	        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
    31	        Ok(super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())), policy)];
    32	    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
    33	        let admission = (|| {
    34	            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
    35	                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
    36	                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
    37	            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
    38	            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
    39	            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
    40	                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
    41	                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
    42	            }
    43	            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
    44	                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
    45	            }
    46	            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
    47	            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
    48	            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
    49	        })();
    50	        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
    51	        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
    52	            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
    53	                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
    54	                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
    55	        }
    56	    }
    57	    out
    58	}
    59	
    60	fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    61	    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
    62	        match t {
    63	            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
    64	            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
    65	            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	    Ok(match rule {
    69	        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
    70	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
    71	        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
    72	        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
    73	        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
    74	        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
    75	        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
    76	        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
    77	            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
    78	            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
    79	        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    80	    })
    81	}

     1	//! Closed enum census: a new IR statement/expression/builtin forces review.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — opaque context changes are blockers.
     3	use snapback4_core::ir::{Expr,Stmt,Builtin};
     4	pub(super) fn body(xs:&[Stmt])->bool{xs.iter().all(|s|match s{
     5	    Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Push{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value),
     6	    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>expr(condition)&&body(then_body)&&body(else_body),
     7	    Stmt::For{..}=>false,
     8	    Stmt::Require{condition,..}|Stmt::Check{condition,..}=>expr(condition),
     9	    Stmt::Insert{row,..}|Stmt::InsertIfAbsent{row,..}|Stmt::Upsert{row,..}=>expr(row),
    10	    Stmt::Update{key,patch,..}=>expr(key)&&expr(patch),
    11	    Stmt::WriteTarget{patch,condition,..}=>expr(patch)&&condition.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|body(&c.setup)&&expr(&c.value)),
    12	    Stmt::Delete{key,..}=>expr(key),Stmt::DeleteTarget{..}|Stmt::Break|Stmt::Continue=>true,
    13	    Stmt::Emit{..}=>false,
    14	})}
    15	fn expr(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{
    16	    Expr::Literal(_)|Expr::Variable(_)|Expr::Input(_)=>true,
    17	    Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base),
    18	    Expr::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().all(expr),Expr::Object(xs)=>xs.values().all(expr),
    19	    Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>expr(base)&&fields.values().all(expr),
    20	    Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>expr(left)&&expr(right),
    21	    Expr::Block{body:xs,value}=>body(xs)&&expr(value),
    22	    Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>r.key.iter().all(expr),
    23	    Expr::Scan(_)|Expr::Merge(_)=>false,
    24	    Expr::Builtin{builtin,args}=>pure(builtin)&&args.iter().all(expr),
    25	}}
    26	fn pure(b:&Builtin)->bool{match b{
    27	    Builtin::JobAs|Builtin::JobQuery{..}=>false,
    28	    Builtin::ProjectFields{..}|Builtin::ReceiptSettled{..}|Builtin::OptionalField{..}|Builtin::Calendar{..}|Builtin::Civil(_)|Builtin::Len|Builtin::Slice|Builtin::Concat|Builtin::Lower|Builtin::Trim|Builtin::Sha256|Builtin::Join|Builtin::Group{..}|Builtin::First|Builtin::Last|Builtin::At|Builtin::Min|Builtin::Max|Builtin::Extremum{..}|Builtin::ExtremumOf{..}|Builtin::Divmod{..}|Builtin::Divide{..}|Builtin::Sort{..}|Builtin::MentionTokens|Builtin::SearchPresent|Builtin::RequireUncapped|Builtin::DeterministicShare|Builtin::Count|Builtin::Sum|Builtin::CountCapped|Builtin::Connected=>true,
    29	}}

     1	//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
     5	// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
     6	const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
     7	pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
     8	pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
     9	    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
    10	}
    11	pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    12	    match op {
    13	        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
    14	        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
    15	        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    16	    }
    17	}
    18	pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    19	    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    20	    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
    21	        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
    22	        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
    23	        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
    24	            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
    25	        } else {edge};
    26	        facts.push(edge);
    27	    }
    28	    RuleExpr::And(facts)
    29	}
    30	
    31	pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    32	    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    33	    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
    34	        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
    35	        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
    36	        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
    37	        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
    38	        _=>None,
    39	    }}
    40	    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
    41	        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
    42	            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
    43	            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
    44	        }
    45	        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
    46	        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    47	    }
    48	    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    49	    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Insert,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    50	    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
    51	}

     1	//! Equal-image substitution only for engine-enforced immutable local fields.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#111-avoid-duplicate-concrete-searches — no reference or mutable-field equality is assumed.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::schema::{Table,Constraint};
     5	
     6	pub(super) fn stable(table:&Table,rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{
     7	    fn term(table:&Table,value:&mut RuleTerm){
     8	        if let RuleTerm::OldField(field)=value && (field=="id"||table.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(field)))) {
     9	            *value=RuleTerm::Field(field.clone());
    10	        }
    11	    }
    12	    fn walk(table:&Table,rule:&mut RuleExpr){match rule{
    13	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)|RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(table,a);term(table,b);},
    14	        RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}|RuleExpr::Created{key,equalities,..}=>{
    15	            for value in key.iter_mut().chain(equalities.iter_mut().map(|(_,t)|t)){term(table,value);}
    16	        },
    17	        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for rule in xs{walk(table,rule)},
    18	        RuleExpr::Not(rule)=>walk(table,rule),
    19	        RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>{},
    20	    }}
    21	    let mut out=rule.clone();walk(table,&mut out);out
    22	}
    23	pub(super) fn update(table:&Table,policy:&RuleExpr)->Result<bool,String>{
    24	    kernel::implies(&stable(table,&table.update_rule_judged()),&stable(table,&policy.old_and_next()))
    25	}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}

codex
I found a concrete advisory gap: the code treats any viewer-keyed existence check containing a string literal as independent delegation authority. That can suppress an escalation note even when the check proves only ownership of an ordinary row. I’m also checking fixture provenance and paired-world construction for false refutations.
codex
The review is NOT READY. Beyond the delegation gap, I found that role advice applies insert intent to updates, unchanged fixture assertion violations can disappear instead of remaining supplemental, synthetic asset failures can fail acceptance without a reachable trace, and the advertised operation-call cap is not enforced globally. I’m checking the exact sites and repairs for the final findings.
codex
**NOT READY.** Four material defects remain.

1. **Ordinary lookups can suppress escalation advice.** [privilege.rs:38](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/privilege.rs:38) treats any viewer-keyed existence probe containing a string literal as a delegation guard.  
   **Counterexample:** self-signup assigns literal `"admin"`, guarded only by existence of the caller’s ordinary project with status `"open"`. That requirement makes `independent` true, suppressing advice even though admin gains protected-table authority.  
   **Repair:** recognize actual role/grant authority, using normalized role-column probes and their relationships; ordinary ownership/status lookups cannot establish delegation.

2. **Authority comparisons apply intent from the wrong effect.** [privilege.rs:37](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/privilege.rs:37) collects insert, update and write invariants together, then applies all of them to each assignment.  
   **Counterexample:** enrollment has insert intent `.role = "member"`; an independently authorized producer updates another principal to `"legal"`. The update receives escalation advice because it violates the unrelated insert invariant.  
   **Repair:** select intent by the executed effect. Analyze upsert insert and update arms separately, applying defaults only to insertion.

3. **Unchanged fixture assertion failures disappear instead of remaining supplemental.** Initial history observation checks reads, while [histories.rs:117](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/histories.rs:117) records assertions only after commits and excludes unchanged pre-existing failures.  
   **Counterexample:** trusted fixture balance is `-1`, with `always balance >= 0`, and the application exposes only queries. Generated balance samples are positive; the actual fixture defect produces no supplemental witness.  
   **Repair:** evaluate assertions on the initial fixture and retain definite failures as `TrustedSeedOnly`. Continue promoting newly violated or changed committed images to authored-trace evidence.

4. **The advertised 8192-call operation budget is not global.** [challenges.rs:356](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges.rs:356) clamps each operation’s quota to at least 16 without a shared remaining-call counter.  
   **Counterexample:** 600 operations with sufficient argument/viewer combinations execute 9600 matrix calls. The total grows with operation count despite the stated cap.  
   **Repair:** enforce a campaign-wide counter and schedule operations fairly within it; explicitly report operations and combinations left unvisited.

The inspected reference-intent path uses authorized engine verdicts and preserves Unknown. Necessary-condition weakening retains connective structure and checks implication; post-commit assertions visit unchanged rows as well. Column worlds include value/presence variants and repeatability checks. Those mechanisms do not erase the findings above, and finite searches or named unsupported boundaries establish no proof.

Static review only; no tests, edits, or qualification claim.
